He was obliged to call to mind the useless expenditure of wishes and hair of the preceding evening, to keep himself from asking that the whole house, and all within it, might be devoured by the flames. He walked on for ten minutes before he became conscious that it was pouring with rain—but the rain, frozen as it was, and even because it was so bitterly cold, seemed to do Thibault good. As the good Magloire had artlessly remarked, his head was on fire.

===Chinese===
“随它去吧！”男爵答道，“我往后还会撞见他的。关于他的种种恶名正四处传扬，偷猎还不过是他被告发的劣迹之一；有人瞧见、并且认出他同一群狼在林子里奔跑——而且那群狼驯服得叫人称奇。依我看，这恶棍未必夜夜都安分地在家待到三更半夜，倒更像是跨在一把扫帚上的次数，比一个正经天主教徒该有的多得多；克鲁瓦约尔（Croyolles）磨坊的主人已经告过他行妖作法的状了。也罢，眼下咱们就不多说这个了；我要叫人去搜他的茅屋，倘若那里头有什么不该有的东西，这妖人的巢穴定要被捣毁，因为我断不能容它在殿下的地界上留存。这会儿，你自己滚吧，越快越好！”

男爵这一通杀气腾腾的长篇申斥，直把鞋匠的怒火激到了顶点；可他到底还是顺着人家让出来的那条道，走出了房间。仗着他在黑暗中能视物的本事，他径直摸到门口，拉开门，跨出了这所房子的门槛——他在这里头留下了那么多缱绻的指望，如今尽数永远失落了；他随手把门砰地一摔，摔得那般狠，整所房子都震了起来。他不得不回想一遍头天晚上白白浪费的那几个愿望和那几根头发，这才勉强按捺住，没有发愿叫这整所房子连同里头所有的一切，统统被烈焰吞噬。他走了足足十分钟，才觉察到外头正下着倾盆大雨——可这雨，虽是冰冷的，甚至正因为冷得这般刺骨，倒仿佛对蒂博颇有好处。正如那位好心的马格卢瓦天真地说的那样，他这颗脑袋正烧得发烫呢。

===Original===
On leaving the Bailiffs house, Thibault had taken the first road he came to; he had no wish to go in one direction more than another, all he wanted was space, fresh air and movement. His desultory walking brought him first of all on to the Value lands; but even then he did not notice where he was until he saw the mill of Croyolles in the distance. He muttered a curse against its fair owner as he passed, rushed on like a madman between Vauciennes and Croyolles, and seeing a dark mass in front of him, plunged into its depths. This dark mass was the forest.

The forest-path to the rear of Ham, which leads from Croyolles to Préciamont, was now ahead of him, and into this he turned, guided solely by chance.

===Chinese===
出了执达吏的宅子，蒂博便顺脚撞上哪条路就走哪条；他并不特别想往哪个方向走，他所要的不过是空旷、新鲜空气和走动罢了。他漫无目的地乱走，先一头撞到了瓦吕（Value）的地界上；可即便在那时，他也还没留意自己身在何处，直到望见远处那座克鲁瓦约尔磨坊。他打那儿经过时，低声咒骂了它那美貌的女主人一句，便发疯一般在沃西安（Vauciennes）与克鲁瓦约尔之间狂奔，瞧见面前一团黑影，便一头扎了进去。这一团黑影，便是那片大森林。

那条通往阿姆（Ham）庄后、连接克鲁瓦约尔与普雷西亚蒙（Préciamont）的林间小径，此刻正在他前头，他便拐了进去，全凭机缘引路。

## Introduction Who Mocquet Was, and How This Tale Became Known to the Narrator / 引言 莫凯是谁，以及这个故事如何传到叙述者耳中
### I
===Original===
Why, I ask myself, during those first twenty years of my literary life, from 1827 to 1847, did I so rarely turn my eyes and thoughts towards the little town where I was born, towards the woods amid which it lies embowered, and the villages that cluster round it? How was it that during all that time the world of my youth seemed to me to have disappeared, as if hidden behind a cloud, whilst the future which lay before me shone clear and resplendent, like those magic islands which Columbus and his companions mistook for baskets of flowers floating on the sea?

===Chinese===
我自问，在我文学生涯最初的二十年里，从 1827 年到 1847 年，我何以那样难得把目光与心思转向我出生的那座小城，转向那座小城掩映其间的那片树林，以及簇拥在它四周的那些村落？那整个时期里，我青春岁月的天地怎就如同消失了一般，仿佛隐没在一重云雾背后，而铺展在我前路之上的未来，却那样清明灿烂，恰似那些魔幻的岛屿——哥伦布和他的同伴们曾把它们错认成漂浮在海面上的花篮？

===Original===
Alas! simply because during the first twenty years of our life, we have Hope for our guide, and during the last twenty, Reality.

===Chinese===
唉！无非是因为，在我们一生的最初二十年里，引领我们的是「希望」，而在最后的二十年里，引领我们的却是「现实」。

===Original===
From the hour when, weary with our journey, we ungird ourselves, and dropping the traveller’s staff, sit down by the wayside, we begin to look back over the road that we have traversed; for it is the way ahead that now is dark and misty, and so we turn and gaze into the depths of the past.

===Chinese===
自那一刻起——我们疲于跋涉，解下腰带，放下旅人的手杖，在路旁坐下——我们便开始回首眺望来时走过的那条路；因为如今昏暗迷蒙的，已是前方的路途，于是我们转过身，凝望向过去的深处。

===Original===
Then with the wide desert awaiting us in front, we are astonished, as we look along the path which we have left behind, to catch sight of first one and then another of those delicious oases of verdure and shade, beside which we never thought of lingering for a moment, and which, indeed we had passed by almost without notice.

===Chinese===
于是，当广阔的荒原在前方等候着我们，我们顺着身后那条小路望去，竟惊讶地发现，先是这一处、继而那一处——那些草木葱茏、浓荫匝地的惬意绿洲；当初我们从它们身旁走过时，从未想过要驻足片刻，事实上，我们几乎是视若无睹地与它们擦肩而过。

===Original===
But, then, how quickly our feet carried us along in those days! we were in such a hurry to reach that goal of happiness, to which no road has ever yet brought any one of us.

===Chinese===
然而，在那样的年岁里，我们的脚步是何等轻快啊！我们如此急切地要奔向那幸福的终点，可世上的任何一条路，还从来不曾把谁真正领到过那里。

===Original===
It is at this point that we begin to see how blind and ungrateful we have been; it is now that we say to ourselves, if we could but once more come across such a green and wooded resting-place, we would stay there for the rest of our lives, would pitch our tent there, and there end our days.

===Chinese===
正是到了这一刻，我们才开始看清自己曾是如何盲目、如何不知感恩；也正是到了此刻，我们才在心底对自己说：倘若能再遇到那样一处绿荫掩映、草木葱茏的歇息之地，我们情愿在那里度过余生，在那里扎下营帐，在那里终了此生。

===Original===
But the body cannot go back and renew its existence, and so memory has to make its pious pilgrimage alone; back to the early days and fresh beginnings of life it travels, like those light vessels that are borne upward by their white sails against the current of a river. Then the body once more pursues its journey; but the body without memory is as the night without stars, as the lamp without its flame.﻿﻿… And so body and memory go their several ways.

===Chinese===
然而肉身既无法折返、重新来过，记忆便只好独自踏上它虔诚的朝圣之旅；它溯流而上，回到生命的最初时光与清新起点，宛如那些扬着白帆、逆着河水轻盈上溯的轻舟。随后，肉身又继续它自己的旅程；可没有了记忆的肉身，恰如没有星辰的夜，恰如没有火焰的灯。……就这样，肉身与记忆，各奔前程。

===Original===
The body, with chance for its guide, moves towards the unknown.

===Chinese===
肉身，以「机缘」为向导，向着未知前行。

===Original===
Memory, that bright will-o’-the-wisp, hovers over the landmarks that are left behind; and memory, we may be sure, will not lose her way. Every oasis is revisited, every association recalled, and then with a rapid flight she returns to the body that grows ever more and more weary, and like the humming of a bee, like the song of a bird, like the murmur of a stream, tells the tale of all that she has seen.

===Chinese===
记忆，那一团明亮的鬼火，在那些被抛在身后的旧路标上空盘桓；而记忆，我们大可放心，她绝不会迷失归途。每一处绿洲她都要重游，每一桩往事她都要唤起，随后她振翅疾飞，回到那副愈来愈疲惫的躯壳身旁，像蜜蜂的嗡嗡，像小鸟的啁啾，像溪流的低语，把一路所见一一道来。

===Original===
And as the tired traveller listens, his eyes grow bright again, his mouth smiles, and a light steals over his face. For Providence in kindness, seeing that he cannot return to youth, allows youth to return to him. And ever after he loves to repeat aloud what memory tells him in her soft, low voice.

===Chinese===
而当这位疲惫的旅人凝神倾听，他的双眼又渐渐放出光来，嘴角浮起笑意，一缕亮色悄然爬上他的面庞。因为仁慈的天意，见他已无法重返青春，便让青春再回到他身边。从此以后，他总爱把记忆用那柔和低回的嗓音所诉与他的一切，朗声重复出来。

===Original===
And is our life, then, bounded by a circle like the earth? Do we, unconsciously, continue to walk towards the spot from which we started? And as we travel nearer and nearer to the grave, do we again draw closer, ever closer, to the cradle?

===Chinese===
那么，我们的一生，难道也像大地一样，被一个圆环所框定？我们是否在不知不觉中，一直朝着出发的那个所在走去？而当我们一步步走向坟墓，是否也正一寸寸地，重新靠近那个摇篮？

### II
===Original===
I cannot say. But what happened to myself, that much at any rate I know. At my first halt along the road of life, my first glance backwards, I began by relating the tale of Bernard and his uncle Berthelin, then the story of Ange Pitou, his fair fiancée, and of Aunt Angélique; after that I told of Conscience and Mariette; and lastly of Catherine Blum and Father Vatrin.

===Chinese===
我说不上来。但发生在我自己身上的事，至少这一点我是清楚的。在人生旅途的第一次驻足、第一回首望时，我先讲述了贝尔纳（Bernard）和他舅舅贝特兰（Berthelin）的故事，接着讲了昂热·皮图（Ange Pitou）、他美丽的未婚妻，还有昂热利克姑姑（Aunt Angélique）的故事；此后我又讲述了《良心》（Conscience）与玛丽埃特（Mariette）；最后是卡特琳·布吕姆（Catherine Blum）与瓦特兰神父（Father Vatrin）。

===Original===
I am now going to tell you the story of Thibault and his wolves, and of the Lord of Vez. And how, you will ask, did I become acquainted with the events which I am now about to bring before you? I will tell you.

===Chinese===
现在，我要给你们讲述的，是蒂博（Thibault）与他那群狼的故事，还有韦兹领主（the Lord of Vez）的故事。你们要问了，我是怎么知晓我将要呈现在你们面前的这些事的呢？我来告诉你们。

===Original===
Have you read my *Mémoires*, and do you remember one, by name Mocquet, who was a friend of my father’s?

===Chinese===
你们可曾读过我的《回忆录》（*Mémoires*）？可还记得其中有一个人，名叫莫凯（Mocquet），是我父亲的一位朋友？

===Original===
If you have read them, you will have some vague recollection of this personage. If you have not read them, you will not remember anything about him at all.

===Chinese===
倘若你们读过，对这个人物总会有些模糊的印象。倘若你们没有读过，那对他自然不会有半点记忆。

===Original===
In either case, then, it is of the first importance that I should bring Mocquet clearly before your mind’s eye.

===Chinese===
那么，无论是哪种情形，我都首先得把莫凯这个人，清晰地呈现在你们的心眼之前。

===Original===
As far back as I can remember, that is when I was about three years of age, we lived, my father and mother and I, in a little Château called Les Fossés, situated on the boundary that separates the departments of Aisne and Oise, between Haramont and Longpré. The little house in question had doubtless been named Les Fossés on account of the deep and broad moat, filled with water, with which it was surrounded.

===Chinese===
就我记忆所能追溯到的最早时候，也就是我大约三岁那年，我们——父亲、母亲和我——住在一座名叫莱福塞（Les Fossés）的小城堡里，它坐落在埃纳省（Aisne）与瓦兹省（Oise）的交界处，介于阿拉蒙（Haramont）与龙普雷（Longpré）之间。这座小小的宅子之所以叫莱福塞，毫无疑问是因为它四周环绕着一条又深又宽、注满了水的壕沟。

===Original===
I do not mention my sister, for she was at school in Paris, and we only saw her once a year, when she was home for a month’s holiday.

===Chinese===
我不提我的姐姐，因为她当时在巴黎上学，我们一年只能见她一次，也就是她回家度假的那一个月。

===Original===
The household, apart from my father, mother and myself, consisted—firstly: of a large black dog, called Truffe, who was a privileged animal and made welcome wherever he appeared, more especially as I regularly went about on his back; secondly: of a gardener, named Pierre, who kept me amply provided with frogs and snakes, two species of living creatures in which I was particularly interested; thirdly: of a negro, a valet of my father’s, named Hippolyte, a sort of black merry-andrew, whom my father, I believe, only kept that he might be well primed with anecdotes wherewith to gain the advantage in his encounters with Brunel1 and beat his wonderful stories; fourthly: of a keeper named Mocquet, for whom I had a great admiration, seeing that he had magnificent stories to tell of ghosts and werewolves, to which I listened every evening, and which were abruptly broken off the instant the General—as my father was usually called—appeared on the scene; fifthly: of a cook, who answered to the name of Marie, but this figure I can no longer recall, it is lost to me in the misty twilight of life; I remember only the name, as given to someone of whom but a shadowy outline remains in my memory, and about whom, as far as I recollect, there was nothing of a very poetic character.

===Chinese===
除了父亲、母亲和我之外，这一家子还有——其一：一条大黑狗，名叫特吕夫（Truffe），它是享有特权的动物，无论走到哪里都受欢迎，尤其因为我惯常是骑在它背上的；其二：一名园丁，名叫皮埃尔（Pierre），他为我源源不断地供给青蛙和蛇，那是我当时格外着迷的两类活物；其三：一个黑人，是父亲的贴身男仆，名叫伊波利特（Hippolyte），一个黑色的丑角式人物，我相信父亲之所以留着他，不过是为了让他肚里装满轶闻趣事，好让父亲在与布鲁内尔（Brunel）交锋时占得上风，压过对方那些天花乱坠的故事；其四：一名看林人，名叫莫凯，我对他仰慕极了，因为他能讲出极精彩的鬼怪与狼人（werewolf）故事，我每晚都去听，而这些故事总在「将军」（the General）——人们通常这样称呼我父亲——一露面的当口就戛然而止；其五：一名厨娘，名叫玛丽（Mararie），但这一个人物我如今已记不真切了，她在我生命中已隐没于那一片朦胧的暮霭；我只记得那个名字，附在某个记忆中仅剩模糊轮廓的人身上，而就我所能忆及的，关于她并没有什么很富诗意的地方。

===Original===
Mocquet, however, is the only person that need occupy our attention for the present. Let me try to make him known to you, both as regards his personal appearance and his character.

===Chinese===
不过，眼下只有莫凯一个人值得我们关注。让我尽力向你们介绍他，从他的外貌到他的性情。

### III
===Original===
Mocquet was a man of about forty years of age, short, thickset, broad of shoulder, and sturdy of leg. His skin was burnt brown by the sun, his eyes were small and piercing, his hair grizzled, and his black whiskers met under his chin in a half circle.

===Chinese===
莫凯是个四十岁上下的汉子，个子不高，敦实粗壮，肩膀宽阔，双腿健壮。他的皮肤被太阳晒得黝黑，眼睛细小而锐利，头发已经花白，黑色的络腮胡在下巴底下连成一道半圆。

===Original===
As I look back, his figure rises before me, wearing a three-cornered hat, and clad in a green waistcoat with silver buttons, velveteen cord breeches, and high leathern gaiters, with a game-bag over his shoulder, his gun in his hand, and a cutty-pipe in his mouth.

===Chinese===
回想起来，他的身影便浮现在我眼前：戴一顶三角帽，身穿一件钉银扣的绿背心、一条丝绒系带马裤，脚蹬高及膝的皮绑腿，肩上挎着猎物袋，手里端着枪，嘴里衔着一杆短烟斗（cutty-pipe）。

===Original===
Let us pause for a moment to consider this pipe, for this pipe grew to be, not merely an accessory, but an integral part of Mocquet. Nobody could remember ever having seen Mocquet without it. If by any chance Mocquet did not happen to have it in his mouth, he had it in his hand.

===Chinese===
让我们稍作停留，细看这杆烟斗，因为这杆烟斗渐渐地已不只是莫凯的一件附属品，而成了他身上不可或缺的一部分。谁也想不起曾见过莫凯没有它的样子。万一莫凯嘴里没有衔着它，那它就一定在他手里。

===Original===
This pipe, having to accompany Mocquet into the heart of the thickest coverts, it was necessary that it should be of such a kind as to offer the least possible opportunity to any other solid body of bringing about its destruction; for the destruction of his old, well-coloured cutty would have been to Mocquet a loss that years alone could have repaired. Therefore the stem of Mocquet’s pipe was not more than half-an-inch long; moreover you might always wager that half that half inch at least was supplied by the quill of a feather.

===Chinese===
这杆烟斗既然要陪莫凯钻进最茂密的树丛深处，它就势必要做成这样一种模样：尽可能少给任何别的硬物以毁坏它的可乘之机；因为那杆烟油熏透的老短烟斗一旦损坏，对莫凯而言将是唯有岁月才能弥补的损失。因此，莫凯那杆烟斗的杆儿，长度不过半英寸；况且你大可打赌，那半英寸里至少有一半，还是用一根羽毛的羽管制成的。

===Original===
This habit of never being without his pipe, which, by causing the almost entire disappearance of both canines, had hollowed out a sort of vice for itself on the left side of his mouth, between the fourth incisor and the first molar, had given rise to another of Mocquet’s habits; this was to speak with his teeth clenched, whereby a certain impression of obstinacy was conveyed by all he said. This became even more marked if Mocquet chanced at any moment to take his pipe out of his mouth, for there was nothing then to prevent the jaws closing and the teeth coming together in a way which prevented the words passing through them at all except in a sort of whistle, which was hardly intelligible.

===Chinese===
这杆烟斗从不离口的习惯，由于使左右两颗犬齿几乎尽数磨没，已在他嘴的左侧、在第四颗门牙与第一颗臼齿之间，为自己磨出了一个安身的凹槽；由此又生出莫凯的另一个习惯，那就是咬紧牙关说话，这让他所说的每一句话都带上了一种近乎执拗的意味。倘若莫凯偶然把烟斗从嘴里取下，这种意味便愈发显著，因为那时再没有什么能阻止他的上下颌合拢、牙齿咬紧，话音便几乎无法从齿缝间穿过，只发出一种近乎哨音的声响，叫人难以听清。

===Original===
Such was Mocquet with respect to outward appearance. In the following pages I will endeavour to give some idea of his intellectual capacity and moral qualities.

===Chinese===
这便是莫凯其人的外貌。在以下几页里，我将努力让大家对他的才智与品性略有所知。

### IV
===Original===
Early one morning, before my father had risen, Mocquet walked into his room, and planted himself at the foot of the bed, stiff and upright as a signpost.

===Chinese===
一天清晨，父亲尚未起身，莫凯便走进了他的卧房，在床脚一站，挺得笔直，像一根路标。

===Original===
“Well, Mocquet,” said my father, “what’s the matter now? what gives me the pleasure of seeing you here at this early hour?”

===Chinese===
「怎么了，莫凯，」父亲说，「又出什么事了？什么事让我大清早就有幸见到你？」

===Original===
“The matter is, General,” replied Mocquet with the utmost gravity, “the matter is that I am *nightmared*.”

===Chinese===
「事情是这样的，将军，」莫凯一脸庄重地答道，「事情就是，我*被魇住了*（*nightmared*）。」

===Original===
Mocquet had, quite unawares to himself, enriched the language with a double verb, both active and passive.

===Chinese===
莫凯在浑然不觉之中，为我们的语言增添了一个兼作主动与被动的双性动词。

===Original===
“You are *nightmared*?” responded my father, raising himself on his elbow. “Dear, dear, that’s a serious matter, my poor Mocquet.”

===Chinese===
「你*被魇住了*？」父亲应道，用胳膊肘撑起身子，「哎呀呀，这可是桩要紧事，我可怜的莫凯。」

===Original===
“You are right there, General.”

===Chinese===
「您说得没错，将军。」

===Original===
And Mocquet took his pipe out of his mouth, a thing he did rarely, and only on the most important occasions.

===Chinese===
说着莫凯把烟斗从嘴里取下——这是他极少做的事，唯有在最要紧的场合才如此。

===Original===
“And how long have you been *nightmared*?” continued my father compassionately.

===Chinese===
「那你*被魇住*有多久了？」父亲怜悯地接着问。

===Original===
“For a whole week, General.”

===Chinese===
「整整一个礼拜了，将军。」

===Original===
“And who by, Mocquet?”

===Chinese===
「是谁魇的你，莫凯？」

===Original===
“Ah! I know very well who by,” answered Mocquet, through his teeth, which were so much the more tightly closed that his pipe was in his hand, and his hand behind his back.

===Chinese===
「哼！我心里明镜似的，知道是谁，」莫凯从齿缝里答道，此刻他的牙咬得格外紧，因为烟斗在他手里，而那只手又背在身后。

===Original===
“And may I also know by whom?”

===Chinese===
「那我也可以知道是谁吗？」

===Original===
“By Mother Durand, of Haramont, who, as you will have heard, is an old witch.”

===Chinese===
「是阿拉蒙的杜朗大妈（Mother Durand），您想必也听说了，她是个老巫婆。」

===Original===
“No, indeed, I assure you I had no idea of such a thing.”

===Chinese===
「没有的事，我向你保证，我对此一无所知。」

===Original===
“Ah! but I know it well enough; I’ve seen her riding past on her broomstick to her Witches’ Sabbath.”

===Chinese===
「哼！我可知道得一清二楚；我亲眼见她骑着扫帚从我跟前掠过，去赶她的巫魔会（Witches’ Sabbath）。」

===Original===
“You have seen her go by on her broomstick?”

===Chinese===
「你亲眼见她骑着扫帚从你跟前过？」

===Original===
“As plainly as I see you, General; and more than that, she has an old black billy-goat at home that she worships.”

===Chinese===
「就跟我现在清清楚楚看见您一样，将军；不止如此，她家里还养着一只老黑公羊，她把它当神明一样供奉着。」

===Original===
“And why should she come and nightmare you?”

===Chinese===
「那她为什么要来魇你呢？」

===Original===
“To revenge herself on me, because I came upon her once at midnight on the heath of Gondreville, when she was dancing round and round in her devil’s circle.”

===Chinese===
「是为了向我报复，因为我有一回半夜在贡德勒维尔（Gondreville）的荒原上撞见了她，那时她正在她的魔鬼圈子里一圈一圈地跳舞。」

===Original===
“This is a most serious accusation which you bring against her, my friend; and before repeating to anyone what you have been telling me in private, I think it would be as well if you tried to collect some more proofs.”

===Chinese===
「你加在她身上的，可是极严重的指控啊，我的朋友；在把私下里对我说的这番话讲给旁人听之前，我看你最好还是再多搜罗些证据才是。」

===Original===
“Proofs! What more proofs do I want! Does not every soul in the village know that in her youth she was the Mistress of Thibault, the wolf-leader?”

===Chinese===
「证据！我还要什么证据！全村上上下下谁不知道，她年轻那会儿，是狼之领主（the wolf-leader）蒂博的情妇？」

===Original===
“Indeed! I must look carefully into this matter, Mocquet.”

===Chinese===
「竟有此事！这件事我可得好好查一查，莫凯。」

===Original===
“I am looking very carefully into it myself, and she shall pay for it, the old mole!”

===Chinese===
「我自己也在仔仔细细地查呢，她得为此付出代价，那只老鼹鼠！」

===Original===
Old mole was an expression that Mocquet had borrowed from his friend Pierre, the gardener, who, as he had no worse enemies to deal with than moles, gave the name of mole to everything and everybody that he particularly detested.

===Chinese===
「老鼹鼠」这个词，是莫凯从他的朋友、园丁皮埃尔那里借来的；皮埃尔因为没有比鼹鼠更可恨的仇敌，便把「鼹鼠」这个名字加诸一切他所格外憎恶的事物和人物。

### V
===Original===
“I must look carefully into this matter”—these words were not said by my father by reason of any belief he had in the truth of Mocquet’s tale about his nightmare; and even the fact of the nightmare being admitted by him, he gave no credence to the idea that it was Mother Durand who had *nightmared* the keeper. Far from it; but my father was not ignorant of the superstitions of the people, and he knew that belief in spells was still widespread among the peasantry in the country districts. He had heard of terrible acts of revenge carried out by the victims on some man or woman who they thought had bewitched them, in the belief that the charm would thus be broken; and Mocquet, while he stood denouncing Mother Durand to my father, had had such an accent of menace in his voice, and had given such a grip to his gun, that my father thought it wise to appear to agree with everything he said, in order to gain his confidence and so prevent him doing anything without first consulting him.

===Chinese===
「这件事我可得好好查一查」——父亲说出这句话，并非出于他对莫凯那套梦魇故事的真实性有什么相信；退一步说，即便承认了那梦魇一事，他也根本不信魇住这看林人的会是杜朗大妈。远非如此；但父亲并非不知道乡民的迷信，他知道在乡间农夫之中，对符咒的信仰依旧广为流传。他曾听说过一些骇人的报复之举——被魇的人对他们自认为是施了魔法的那个人，不论是男是女，痛下毒手，因为他们深信唯有如此才能破除那道邪咒；而当莫凯站在父亲面前痛斥杜朗大妈时，他话音里带着那样一种威胁的口气，又把猎枪攥得那样紧，以至于父亲认为，明智之举是装作对他所说的一切都深以为然，好借此赢取他的信任，免得他不经通报便擅自妄为。

===Original===
So, thinking that he had so far gained an influence over Mocquet, my father ventured to say:

===Chinese===
于是，父亲自以为已经对莫凯有了几分影响力，便试探着说：

===Original===
“But before you make her pay for it, my good Mocquet, you ought to be quite sure that no one can cure you of your nightmare.”

===Chinese===
「不过，在你让她付出代价之前，我亲爱的莫凯，你总该先弄个明白：确实没有谁能治得好你这魇病了吧？」

===Original===
“No one can cure me, General,” replied Mocquet in a tone of conviction.

===Chinese===
「没人治得好我，将军，」莫凯以一种深信不疑的口吻答道。

===Original===
“How! No one able to cure you?”

===Chinese===
「怎么！没人能治得好你？」

===Original===
“No one; I have tried the impossible.”

===Chinese===
「没人；我什么办法都试过了。」

===Original===
“And how did you try?”

===Chinese===
「你是怎么试的？」

===Original===
“First of all, I drank a large bowl of hot wine before going to bed.”

===Chinese===
「头一桩，我睡前灌下一大碗滚烫的酒。」

===Original===
“And who recommended that remedy? was it Monsieur Lécosse?” Monsieur Lécosse was the doctor in repute at Villers-Cotterets.

===Chinese===
「是谁给你开的这个方子？是勒科斯先生（Monsieur Lécosse）吗？」勒科斯先生是维莱-科特雷（Villers-Cotterets）一带有名望的医生。

===Original===
“Monsieur Lécosse?” exclaimed Mocquet. “No, indeed! What should he know about spells! By my faith, no! it was not Monsieur Lécosse.”

===Chinese===
「勒科斯先生？」莫凯叫起来，「哪里的话！他懂什么符咒！凭良心说，不是他！可不是勒科斯先生。」

===Original===
“Who was it, then?”

===Chinese===
「那又是谁呢？」

===Original===
“It was the shepherd of Longpré.”

===Chinese===
「是龙普雷那个牧羊人。」

===Original===
“But a bowl of wine, you dunderhead! Why, you must have been dead drunk.”

===Chinese===
「可一碗酒，你这榆木脑袋！那你岂不是喝得烂醉如泥了。」

===Original===
“The shepherd drank half of it.”

===Chinese===
「那牧羊人陪我喝了半碗。」

===Original===
“I see; now I understand why he prescribed it. And did the bowl of wine have any effect?”

===Chinese===
「我明白了；现在我懂他为什么开这个方子了。那这一碗酒可曾有些效用？」

===Original===
“Not any, General; she came trampling over my chest that night, just as if I had taken nothing.”

===Chinese===
「一点也没有，将军；那天夜里她照样踩着我的胸口蹦跶，就跟什么也没喝一样。」

===Original===
“And what did you do next? You were not obliged, I suppose, to limit your efforts to your bowl of hot wine?”

===Chinese===
「那后来你又如何？你总不见得非得把你那一套本事都耗在一碗热酒上吧？」

===Original===
“I did what I do when I want to catch a *wily beast*.”

===Chinese===
「我使的是我想要捉一只*狡兽*（*wily beast*）时使的那一手。」

===Original===
Mocquet made use of a phraseology which was all his own; no one had ever succeeded in inducing him to say a *wild beast*; every time my father said *wild beast*, Mocquet would answer, “Yes, General, I know, a *wily beast*.”

===Chinese===
莫凯用的是一套只属于他自己的措辞；从来没有人能说服他把「野兽」（*wild beast*）说成「野兽」；每逢父亲说「野兽」，莫凯总要应上一句：「是，将军，我知道，一只『狡兽』（*wily beast*）。」

===Original===
“You still stick to your *wily beast*, then?” my father said to him on one occasion.

===Chinese===
「这么说，你还是咬定你的『狡兽』不放？」有一次父亲对他说。

===Original===
“Yes, General, but not out of obstinacy.”

===Chinese===
「是的，将军，可这并不是出于拗性子。」

===Original===
“And why then, may I ask?”

===Chinese===
「那敢问又是为什么呢？」

===Original===
“Because, General, with all due respect to you, you are mistaken about it.”

===Chinese===
「因为，将军，恕我冒犯，在这件事上您弄错了。」

===Original===
“Mistaken? I? How?”

===Chinese===
「弄错了？我？怎么错了？」

===Original===
“Because you ought not to say a *wild beast*, but a *wily beast*.”

===Chinese===
「因为您不该说『野兽』，而该说『狡兽』。」

===Original===
“And what is a *wily beast*, Mocquet?”

===Chinese===
「那什么叫『狡兽』呢，莫凯？」

===Original===
“It is an animal that only goes about at night; that is, an animal that creeps into the pigeon-houses and kills the pigeons, like the polecat, or into the chicken-houses, to kill the chickens, like the fox; or into the folds, to kill the sheep, like the wolf; it means an animal which is cunning and deceitful, in short, a *wily beast*.”

===Chinese===
「那是一种只在夜里出来走动的动物；也就是说，一种溜进鸽棚去咬鸽子的动物，比如鸡鼬；或者溜进鸡舍去咬鸡的，比如狐狸；又或者溜进羊圈去咬羊的，比如狼；它的意思是一种既狡猾又阴险的动物，简而言之，一只『狡兽』。」

===Original===
It was impossible to find anything to say after such a logical definition as this.—My father, therefore, remained silent, and Mocquet, feeling that he had gained a victory, continued to call *wild beasts*, *wily beasts*, utterly unable to understand my father’s obstinacy in continuing to call *wily beasts*, *wild beasts*.

===Chinese===
面对如此合乎逻辑的定义，实在再无可辩驳之言。——于是父亲默然不语，而莫凯自觉胜券在握，继续管「野兽」叫「狡兽」，对我父亲竟还拗着把「狡兽」叫作「野兽」百思不得其解。

===Original===
So now you understand why, when my father asked him what else he had done, Mocquet answered, “I did what I do when I want to catch a *wily beast*.”

===Chinese===
那么诸位现在便明白，父亲问他此外还做了些什么时，莫凯为何答道：「我使的是我想要捉一只*狡兽*时使的那一手。」

===Original===
We have interrupted the conversation to give this explanation; but as there was no need of explanation between my father and Mocquet, they had gone on talking, you must understand, without any such break.

===Chinese===
我们这里打断了对话，特作这番交代；但父亲与莫凯之间原无需任何解释，这一点诸位须明白，他们其实就这样不间断地一直谈了下去。

### VI
===Original===
“And what is it you do, Mocquet, when you want to catch this animal of yours?” asked my father.

===Chinese===
「那么你要捉你那畜生时，是怎么个捉法呢，莫凯？」父亲问。

===Original===
“I set a *trarp*, General.” Mocquet always called a trap a trarp.

===Chinese===
「我下个*陷夹*（*trarp*），将军。」莫凯总把陷阱（trap）说成「陷夹」（trarp）。

===Original===
“Do you mean to tell me you have set a trap to catch Mother Durand?”

===Chinese===
「你是想告诉我，你下了个陷阱去捉杜朗大妈？」

===Original===
My father had of course said *trap*; but Mocquet did not like anyone to pronounce words differently from himself, so he went on:

===Chinese===
父亲说的自然是「陷阱」二字；可莫凯见不得旁人把字眼念得跟他不一样，于是他接着道：

===Original===
“Just so, General; I have set a *trarp* for Mother Durand.”

===Chinese===
「正是如此，将军；我给杜朗大妈下了个*陷夹*。」

===Original===
“And where have you put your *trarp*? Outside your door?”

===Chinese===
「那你这*陷夹*下在哪儿了？门外吗？」

===Original===
My father, you see, was willing to make concessions.

===Chinese===
诸位看到了吧，父亲是肯让步的。

===Original===
“Outside my door! Much good that would be! I only know she gets into my room, but I cannot even guess which way she comes.”

===Chinese===
「门外！那能顶什么用！我只知道她钻进了我房里，可她究竟从哪儿进来，我连猜都猜不着。」

===Original===
“Down the chimney, perhaps?”

===Chinese===
「莫不是从烟囱下来的？」

===Original===
“There is no chimney, and besides, I never see her until I feel her.”

===Chinese===
「压根儿没有烟囱，再说，我总是先感觉到她，才能看见她。」

===Original===
“And you do see her, then?”

===Chinese===
「这么说，你确实看见她了？」

===Original===
“As plainly I see you, General.”

===Chinese===
「就跟我现在清清楚楚看见您一样，将军。」

===Original===
“And what does she do?”

===Chinese===
「那她都干些什么？」

===Original===
“Nothing agreeable, you may be sure; she tramples all over my chest: thud, thud! thump, thump!”

===Chinese===
「您放心，没什么舒坦的；她在我胸口一顿乱踩：咚，咚！嘭，嘭！」

===Original===
“Well, where *have* you set your trap, then?”

===Chinese===
「那你这陷阱到底下在哪儿了？」

===Original===
“The *trarp*, why, I put it on my own stomach.”

===Chinese===
「*陷夹*么，嗨，我把它搁在了自己肚皮上。」

===Original===
“And what kind of a *trarp* did you use?”

===Chinese===
「那你用的是一副什么样的*陷夹*？」

===Original===
“Oh! a first-rate *trarp*!”

===Chinese===
「噢！一副顶呱呱的*陷夹*！」

===Original===
“What was it?”

===Chinese===
「什么样的？」

===Original===
“The one I made to catch the grey wolf with, that used to kill M. Destournelles’ sheep.”

===Chinese===
「就是我做来捉那头灰色恶狼的那一副，那狼专爱咬德图尔内勒先生（M. Destournelles）的羊。」

===Original===
“Not such a first-rate one, then, for the grey wolf ate up your bait, and then bolted.”

===Chinese===
「那可不见得顶呱呱吧——那头灰狼把你的饵吃了，然后一溜烟跑了。」

===Original===
“You know why he was not caught, General.”

===Chinese===
「您知道它为什么没被捉住的，将军。」

===Original===
“No, I do not.”

===Chinese===
「不，我不知道。」

===Original===
“Because it was the black wolf that belonged to old Thibault, the sabot-maker.”

===Chinese===
「因为那是老木屐匠蒂博的那头黑狼。」

===Original===
“It could not have been Thibault’s black wolf, for you said yourself just this moment that the wolf that used to come and kill M. Destournelles’ sheep was a grey one.”

===Chinese===
「那不可能是蒂博的黑狼，因为方才你自己还说过，常来咬德图尔内勒先生羊群的那头狼是灰色的。」

===Original===
“He is grey now, General; but thirty years ago, when Thibault the sabot-maker was alive, he was black; and, to assure you of the truth of this, look at my hair, which was black as a raven’s thirty years ago, and now is as grey as the Doctor’s.”

===Chinese===
「它现在是灰的，将军；可三十年前，木屐匠蒂博还在世的时候，它是黑的；为了向您担保这话不假，您瞧瞧我的头发，三十年前黑得跟乌鸦一样，如今却已花白得跟『医生』一个样了。」

===Original===
The Doctor was a cat, an animal of some fame, that you will find mentioned in my *Mémoires* and known as the Doctor on account of the magnificent fur which nature had given it for a coat.

===Chinese===
「医生」是一只猫，颇有些名头，在我的《回忆录》里也能查到它；它之所以被叫作「医生」，全因造物赏了它一身华丽无比的皮毛。

===Original===
“Yes,” replied my father, “I know your tale about Thibault, the sabot-maker; but, if the black wolf is the devil, Mocquet, as you say he is, he would not change colour.”

===Chinese===
「不错，」父亲应道，「我知道你那套关于木屐匠蒂博的说法；不过，莫凯，倘若那头黑狼如你所说就是魔鬼，它便不会改换颜色。」

===Original===
“Not at all, General; only it takes him a hundred years to become quite white, and the last midnight of every hundred years, he turns black as a coal again.”

===Chinese===
「一点也不，将军；只不过它要花上一百年才能变得通体雪白，而每一百年的最后一个午夜，它又会重新变得像煤一样黑。」

===Original===
“I give up the case, then, Mocquet: all I ask is, that you will not tell my son this fine tale of yours, until he is fifteen at least.”

===Chinese===
「那么这桩案子我便不再追究了，莫凯：我只求你一件事——在我儿子至少满十五岁之前，别把你这美妙的故事讲给他听。」

===Original===
“And why, General?”

===Chinese===
「这是为何，将军？」

===Original===
“Because it is no use stuffing his mind with nonsense of that kind, until he is old enough to laugh at wolves, whether they are white, grey or black.”

===Chinese===
「因为在他长到能对狼一笑置之前——管它是白是灰是黑——拿这种不着边际的胡话去塞满他的脑袋，是全无益处的。」

===Original===
“It shall be as you say, General; he shall hear nothing of this matter.”

===Chinese===
「一切照您吩咐，将军；关于这件事，他一个字也不会听见。」

===Original===
“Go on, then.”

===Chinese===
「那你说下去。」

===Original===
“Where had we got to, General?”

===Chinese===
「我们刚才说到哪儿了，将军？」

===Original===
“We had got to your *trarp*, which you had put on your stomach, and you were saying that it was a first-rate *trarp*.”

===Chinese===
「说到你那副*陷夹*，你把它搁在了肚皮上，还夸它是一副顶呱呱的*陷夹*。」

===Original===
“By my faith, General, that was a first-rate *trarp*! It weighed a good ten pounds. What am I saying! fifteen pounds at least with its chain!”

===Chinese===
「凭良心说，将军，那可真是一副顶呱呱的*陷夹*！足有十磅重。我说什么呀！连铁链算在内，至少十五磅！」

===Original===
I put the chain over my wrist.

===Chinese===
「我把铁链缠在手腕上。」

===Original===
“And what happened that night?”

===Chinese===
「那夜又如何呢？」

===Original===
“That night? why, it was worse than ever! Generally, it was in her leather overshoes she came and kneaded my chest, but that night she came in her wooden sabots.”

===Chinese===
「那夜？嘿，比哪一夜都凶！往常她来踩我的胸口，穿的是皮套鞋，可那一夜她穿的是木屐。」

===Original===
“And she comes like this…?”

===Chinese===
「她就这样每夜都来……？」

===Original===
“Every blessed one of God’s nights, and it is making me quite thin; you can see for yourself, General, I am growing as thin as a lath. However, this morning I made up my mind.”

===Chinese===
「上帝赐下的每一个夜晚，一个不落，这事可把我折腾瘦了；您自己瞧得见，将军，我瘦得像根板条了。不过，今天早上我拿定了主意。」

===Original===
“And what did you decide upon, Mocquet?”

===Chinese===
「你拿定了什么主意，莫凯？」

===Original===
“Well, then, I made up my mind I would let fly at her with my gun.”

===Chinese===
「是这样的，我打定主意，要照她开上一枪。」

===Original===
“That was a wise decision to come to. And when do you think of carrying it out?”

===Chinese===
「这倒是个明智的决定。那你打算什么时候动手？」

===Original===
“This evening, or tomorrow at latest, General.”

===Chinese===
「今儿晚上，至迟明天，将军。」

===Original===
“Confound it! And just as I was wanting to send you over to Villers-Hellon.”

===Chinese===
「见鬼！偏偏我正想差你去一趟维莱-埃隆（Villers-Hellon）。」

===Original===
“That won’t matter, General. Was it something that you wanted done at once?”

===Chinese===
「这不要紧，将军。是有什么要紧事非得马上办吗？」

===Original===
“Yes, at once.”

===Chinese===
「是的，马上要办。」

===Original===
“Very well, then, I can go over to Villers-Hellon—it’s not above a few miles, if I go through the wood—and get back here this evening; the journey both ways is only twenty-four miles, and we have covered a few more than that before now out shooting, General.”

===Chinese===
「那好，我可以去一趟维莱-埃隆——要是我穿过林子走，也不过几英里的路——今晚就能赶回来；来回加起来也不过二十四英里，从前我们出门打猎，走的路可比这还多上几英里呢，将军。」

===Original===
“That’s settled, then; I will write a letter for you to give to M. Collard, and then you can start.”

===Chinese===
「那就这么定了；我给你写封信，你带去给科拉尔先生（M. Collard），然后你就可以动身了。」

===Original===
“I will start, General, without a moment’s delay.”

===Chinese===
「我马上动身，将军，一刻也不耽搁。」

===Original===
My father rose, and wrote to M. Collard; the letter was as follows:

===Chinese===
父亲起身，给科拉尔先生写了一封信；信文如下：

===Original===
> “My dear Collard,
> “I am sending you that idiot of a keeper of mine, whom you know; he has taken into his head that an old woman *nightmares* him every night, and, to rid himself of this vampire, he intends nothing more nor less than to kill her.
> “Justice, however, might not look favourably on this method of his for curing himself of indigestion, and so I am going to start him off to you on a pretext of some kind or other. Will you, also, on some pretext or other, send him on, as soon as he gets to you, to Danré, at Vouty, who will send him on to Dulauloy, who, with or without pretext, may then, as far as I care, send him on to the devil?
> “In short, he must be kept going for a fortnight at least. By that time we shall have moved out of here and shall be at Antilly, and as he will then no longer be in the district of Haramont, and as his nightmare will probably have left him on the way, Mother Durand will be able to sleep in peace, which I should certainly not advise her to do if Mocquet were remaining anywhere in her neighbourhood.
> “He is bringing you six brace of snipe and a hare, which we shot while out yesterday on the marshes of Vallue.
> “A thousand-and-one of my tenderest remembrances to the fair Herminie, and as many kisses to the dear little Caroline.
> “Your friend,

===Chinese===
> 「我亲爱的科拉尔：
> 「我把我那个白痴看林人给你送去，你认得他的；他忽然动了念头，认定有个老太婆每夜来魇他，为了摆脱这个吸血鬼，他打算干脆把她杀了。
> 「不过，法律对他这种医治『消化不良』的法子，怕是不大能另眼相看的，所以我打算找个什么由头把他打发到你那儿去。也请你随便找个由头，他一到你那儿便把他转送给武蒂（Vouty）的当雷（Danré），当雷再把他转送给迪洛卢瓦（Dulauloy），迪洛卢瓦呢，有无由头皆可，尽可把他打发去见鬼，我才不管呢。
> 「总之，必须让他在外头至少转悠上半个月。届时我们便已搬离此地、住到昂蒂伊（Antilly）去了，而他既然不再身处阿拉蒙一带，又多半在半路上就甩掉了那魇病，杜朗大妈也就可以安然入睡了——当然，倘若莫凯还留在她左近，我可是断然不会劝她安然入睡的。
> 「他给你捎去六对鹬鸟和一只野兔，是我们昨天在瓦吕（Vallue）的沼泽上打猎时猎得的。
> 「代我向美丽的埃尔米妮（Herminie）献上一千零一个最温柔的致意，再替我吻可爱的小卡罗琳（Caroline）同样那么多下。
> 「你的朋友，

===Original===
 “**Alex. Dumas**.”

===Chinese===
 **亚历·大仲马**（**Alex. Dumas**）。」

===Original===
An hour later Mocquet was on his way, and, at the end of three weeks, he rejoined us at Antilly.

===Chinese===
一小时后莫凯便启程了，三个礼拜之后，他在昂蒂伊与我们重新会合。

===Original===
“Well,” asked my father, seeing him reappear in robust health, “well, and how about Mother Durand?”

===Chinese===
「怎么样，」父亲见他重新露面时红光满面、身体硬朗，便问道，「怎么样，杜朗大妈的事呢？」

===Original===
“Well, General,” replied Mocquet cheerfully, “I’ve got rid of the old mole; it seems she has no power except in her own district.”

===Chinese===
「嘿，将军，」莫凯兴高采烈地答道，「我甩掉那只老鼹鼠了；看来她的法力只在自己那一带才管用。」

### VII
===Original===
Twelve years had passed since Mocquet’s nightmare, and I was now over fifteen years of age. It was the winter of 1817 to 1818; ten years before that date I had, alas! lost my father.

===Chinese===
自从莫凯那场魇病，一晃十二年过去了，我也已过了十五岁。那是 1817 年到 1818 年的冬天；比那再早十年，唉！我失去了父亲。

===Original===
We no longer had a Pierre for gardener, a Hippolyte for valet, or a Mocquet for keeper; we no longer lived at the Château of Les Fossés or in the villa at Antilly, but in the marketplace of Villers Cotterets, in a little house opposite the fountain, where my mother kept a *bureau de tabac*, selling powder and shot as well over the same counter.

===Chinese===
我们再也没有皮埃尔做园丁、伊波利特做贴身男仆、莫凯做看林人了；我们也不再住在莱福塞的城堡，也不在昂蒂伊的别墅，而是住在维莱-科特雷集市广场上的一所小房子里，正对着那口喷泉；母亲在那里开着一家烟草铺（*bureau de tabac*），在同一张柜台上兼售火药和弹丸。

===Original===
As you have already read in my *Mémoires*, although still young, I was an enthusiastic sportsman. As far as sport went, however, that is according to the usual acceptation of the word, I had none, except when my cousin, M. Deviolaine, the ranger of the forest at Villers-Cotterets, was kind enough to ask leave of my mother to take me with him. I filled up the remainder of my time with poaching.

===Chinese===
诸位在我的《回忆录》里已经读到，我虽年纪尚轻，却已是一个热衷的猎手。不过严格说来，按这个词通常的意义来论「打猎」，我是没有份的——唯有我表兄、维莱-科特雷森林的林务官德维奥莱纳先生（M. Deviolaine）好心地向我母亲求得允许、带我一同出去时，才算例外。余下的时间，我便以偷猎来打发。

===Original===
For this double function of sportsman and poacher I was well provided with a delightful single-barrelled gun, on which was engraven the monogram of the Princess Borghese, to whom it had originally belonged. My father had given it me when I was a child, and when, after his death, everything had to be sold, I implored so urgently to be allowed to keep my gun, that it was not sold with the other weapons, and the horses and carriages.

===Chinese===
为了这「猎手兼偷猎者」的双重身份，我手下有一杆称心如意的单管猎枪，枪上刻着博尔盖塞公主（Princess Borghese）的花体字母缩写，这杆枪原是她的物事。这枪是父亲在我孩提时赠我的；待他身后、万事皆需变卖之时，我苦苦哀求留下我的枪，于是它才没有随其他武器、马匹与车马一同被卖掉。

===Original===
The most enjoyable time for me was the winter; then the snow lay on the ground, and the birds, in their search for food, were ready to come wherever grain was sprinkled for them. Some of my father’s old friends had fine gardens, and I was at liberty to go and shoot the birds there as I liked. So I used to sweep the snow away, spread some grain, and, hiding myself within easy gunshot, fire at the birds, sometimes killing six, eight, or even ten at a time.

===Chinese===
对我而言最惬意的是冬天；那时地上覆着积雪，鸟儿们四处觅食，凡是撒下谷粒的地方，它们都肯飞来。父亲几位老友的宅子里有大花园，我可以随意到那里去打鸟。于是我便扫开一片雪，撒上些谷粒，自己藏在枪膛射程之内，待鸟群飞来便开枪，有时一枪便打下六只、八只，乃至十只。

===Original===
Then, if the snow lasted, there was another thing to look forward to—the chance of tracing a wolf to its lair, and a wolf so traced was everybody’s property. The wolf, being a public enemy, a murderer beyond the pale of the law, might be shot at by all or anyone, and so, in spite of my mother’s cries, who dreaded the double danger for me, you need not ask if I seized my gun, and was first on the spot ready for sport.

===Chinese===
再者，倘若积雪不化，便另有一桩可盼之事——那便是循着踪迹追到一头狼的巢穴，而这样一头被追踪到的狼，便是人人得而诛之的。狼既是公敌，是法外之凶徒，人人皆可向它开枪；因此，尽管母亲连声惊呼、唯恐我遭那双重凶险，诸位也不必问我是不是抓起了猎枪、头一个赶到现场待命出猎了。

===Original===
The winter of 1817 to 1818 had been long and severe; the snow was lying a foot deep on the ground, and so hard frozen that it had held for a fortnight past, and still there were no tidings of anything.

===Chinese===
1817 年到 1818 年的那个冬天，漫长而酷寒；积雪在地上足有一尺厚，又冻得结实，已经持续了半个月之久，却仍没有任何动静。

===Original===
Towards four o’clock one afternoon Mocquet called upon us; he had come to lay in his stock of powder. While so doing, he looked at me and winked with one eye. When he went out, I followed.

===Chinese===
一天下午将近四点，莫凯上门来了；他是来采办火药的。这当口，他瞅着我，冲我眨了眨一只眼。他出门时，我便跟了出去。

===Original===
“What is it, Mocquet?” I asked, “tell me.”

===Chinese===
「什么事，莫凯？」我问，「告诉我吧。」

===Original===
“Can’t you guess, Monsieur Alexandre?”

===Chinese===
「您猜不着么，亚历山大先生（Monsieur Alexandre）？」

===Original===
“No, Mocquet.”

===Chinese===
「猜不着，莫凯。」

===Original===
“You don’t guess, then, that if I come and buy powder here from Madame, your mother, instead of going to Haramont for it—in short, if I walk three miles instead of only a quarter that distance, that I might possibly have a bit of a shoot to propose to you?”

===Chinese===
「那您就没琢磨琢磨，我若不到阿拉蒙去买火药，偏要上您母亲夫人这里来买——一句话，我宁肯走三英里，也不肯只走那三英里的四分之一，是不是想邀您一块儿去打上一阵？」

===Original===
“Oh, you good Mocquet! and what and where?”

===Chinese===
「哦，好莫凯！打什么？上哪儿打？」

===Original===
“There’s a wolf, Monsieur Alexandre.”

===Chinese===
「有一头狼，亚历山大先生。」

===Original===
“Not really?”

===Chinese===
「当真？」

===Original===
“He carried off one of M. Destournelles’ sheep last night, I have traced him to the Tillet woods.”

===Chinese===
「昨儿夜里它叼走了德图尔内勒先生一只羊，我循着踪迹追到了蒂莱（Tillet）树林。」

===Original===
“And what then?”

===Chinese===
「然后呢？」

===Original===
“Why then, I am certain to see him again tonight, and shall find out where his lair is, and tomorrow morning we’ll finish his business for him.”

===Chinese===
「然后嘛，今夜它必定还会露面，我就能摸清它的老巢在哪儿，明天一早，咱们便给它送终。」

===Original===
“Oh, this is luck!”

===Chinese===
「哦，这可真是运气！」

===Original===
“Only, we must first ask leave.…”

===Chinese===
「只是，咱们得先求得许可……」

===Original===
“Of whom, Mocquet?”

===Chinese===
「求谁，莫凯？」

===Original===
“Leave of Madame.”

===Chinese===
「求夫人。」

===Original===
“All right, come in, then, we will ask her at once.”

===Chinese===
「好，那就进来吧，我们这便去问她。」

===Original===
My mother had been watching us through the window; she suspected that some plot was hatching between us.

===Chinese===
母亲一直隔着窗子盯着我们；她疑心我们俩在捣鼓什么鬼。

===Original===
“I have no patience with you, Mocquet,” she said, as we went in, “you have no sense or discretion.”

===Chinese===
「我对你真是没了耐心，莫凯，」我们进屋时她说，「你一点分寸和忌讳都没有。」

===Original===
“In what way, Madame?” asked Mocquet.

===Chinese===
「这话从何说起，夫人？」莫凯问。

===Original===
“To go exciting him in the way you do; he thinks too much of sport as it is.”

===Chinese===
「这样去撩拨他；他本来就太把打猎当回事了。」

===Original===
“Nay, Madame, it is with him, as with dogs of breed; his father was a sportsman, he is a sportsman, and his son will be a sportsman after him; you must make up your mind to that.”

===Chinese===
「嗳，夫人，他这情形啊，跟那些名种猎犬一个样；他父亲是猎人，他就是猎人，往后他的儿子也还是猎人；您可得把心放宽了认下这一桩。」

===Original===
“And supposing some harm should come to him?”

===Chinese===
「可万一他出了什么事呢？」

===Original===
“Harm come to him with me? With Mocquet? No, indeed! I will answer for it with my own life, that he shall be safe. Harm happen to him, to him, the General’s son? Never, never, never!”

===Chinese===
「他跟着我会出事？跟着我莫凯？断断不会！我愿拿自己这条命担保，他一定安然无恙。他会出事，他，将军的儿子？绝不，绝不，绝不！」

===Original===
But my poor mother shook her head; I went to her and flung my arms round her neck.

===Chinese===
可怜的母亲只是摇头；我走到她跟前，一把搂住她的脖子。

===Original===
“Mother, dearest,” I cried, “please let me go.”

===Chinese===
「最亲爱的妈妈，」我喊道，「求您让我去吧。」

===Original===
“You will load his gun for him, then, Mocquet?”

===Chinese===
「那你会替他往枪里装药吗，莫凯？」

===Original===
“Have no fear, sixty grains of powder, not a grain more or less, and a twenty to the pound bullet.”

===Chinese===
「您只管放心，整整六十格令火药，不多一粒不少一粒，再加一颗二十颗一磅的弹丸。」

===Original===
“And you will not leave him?”

===Chinese===
「你不离开他？」

===Original===
“I will stay by him like his shadow.”

===Chinese===
「我如影随形地守着他。」

===Original===
“You will keep him near you?”

===Chinese===
「你让他紧跟着你？」

===Original===
“Between my legs.”

===Chinese===
「就在我两腿之间。」

===Original===
“I give him into your sole charge, Mocquet.”

===Chinese===
「那我就把他全权交给你了，莫凯。」

===Original===
“And he shall be given back to you safe and sound. Now, Monsieur Alexandre, gather up your traps, and let us be off; your mother has given her permission.”

===Chinese===
「我保准把他毫发无伤地还给您。好了，亚历山大先生，收拾您的家伙，咱们走吧；令堂已经应允了。」

===Original===
“You are not taking him away this evening, Mocquet.”

===Chinese===
「你今晚不就把他带走了吧，莫凯。」

===Original===
“I must, Madame, tomorrow morning will be too late to fetch him; we must hunt the wolf at dawn.”

===Chinese===
「我非得今晚不可，夫人，明天一早再去接他就太迟了；我们得在破晓时分围猎那头狼。」

===Original===
“The wolf! it is for a wolf-hunt that you are asking for him to go with you?”

===Chinese===
「狼！你要他同去，是为了一场猎狼？」

===Original===
“Are you afraid that the wolf will eat him?”

===Chinese===
「您是怕那狼把他吃了么？」

===Original===
“Mocquet! Mocquet!”

===Chinese===
「莫凯！莫凯！」

===Original===
“But when I tell you that I will be answerable for everything!”

===Chinese===
「可我都说了，一切由我一力担待！」

===Original===
“And where will the poor child sleep?”

===Chinese===
「那可怜的孩子在哪睡呢？」

===Original===
“With father Mocquet, of course, he will have a good mattress laid on the floor, and sheets white as those which God has spread over the fields, and two good warm coverlids; I promise you that he shall not catch cold.”

===Chinese===
「当然跟莫凯爹爹一起睡啦，我会给他地上铺一张厚实的床垫，再给他铺上雪白的床单——白得就像上帝铺在田野上的那层雪——还有两床暖和的厚被子；我向您保证，他绝不会着凉。」

===Original===
“I shall be all right, mother, you may be sure! Now then, Mocquet, I am ready.”

===Chinese===
「我会没事的，妈妈，您放心好啦！那么，莫凯，我准备好了。」

===Original===
“And you don’t even give me a kiss, you poor boy, you!”

===Chinese===
「你这可怜的孩子，连亲都不亲我一下！」

===Original===
“Indeed, yes, dear mother, and a good many more than one!”

===Chinese===
「怎么会不亲，亲爱的妈妈，何止一下，要亲上好些下呢！」

===Original===
And I threw myself on my mother’s neck, stifling her with my caresses as I clasped her in my arms.

===Chinese===
说着，我扑到母亲颈间，把她紧紧搂在怀里，一阵亲昵几乎让她喘不过气来。

===Original===
“And when shall I see you again?”

===Chinese===
「那我什么时候能再见到你？」

===Original===
“Oh, do not be uneasy if he does not return before tomorrow evening.”

===Chinese===
「哦，夫人不必挂心，他若到明晚才回来，您也别慌。」

===Original===
“How, tomorrow evening! and you spoke of starting at dawn!”

===Chinese===
「怎么，明晚！你刚才还说要破晓动身呢！」

===Original===
“At dawn for the wolf; but if we miss him, the lad must have a shot or two at the wild ducks on the marshes of Vallue.”

===Chinese===
「破晓是去围那头狼；可万一扑了空，这孩子总该在瓦吕的沼泽上朝野鸭子放上几枪才是。」

===Original===
“I see! you are going to drown him for me!”

===Chinese===
「我懂了！你是要替我把他淹死！」

===Original===
“By the name of all that’s good, Madame, if I was not speaking to the General’s widow—I should say—”

===Chinese===
「凭一切神圣之物起誓，夫人，若不是我此刻正对着将军的遗孀说话——我倒要说——」

===Original===
“What Mocquet? What would you say?”

===Chinese===
「说什么，莫凯？你要说什么？」

===Original===
“That you will make nothing but a wretched milksop of your boy.… If the General’s mother had been always behind him, pulling at his coattails, as you are behind this child, he would never even have had the courage to cross the sea to France.”

===Chinese===
「我要说，您这样下去，只会把您儿子养成一个没出息的娘娘腔。……倘若将军的母亲当年也像您拉扯这孩子这样，老在将军身后扯着他的衣后摆，那他怕是一辈子也生不出渡海来到法兰西的那股子胆气了。」

===Original===
“You are right, Mocquet! take him away! I am a poor fool.”

===Chinese===
「你说得对，莫凯！带他走吧！我是个可怜的糊涂虫。」

===Original===
And my mother turned aside, to wipe away a tear.

===Chinese===
母亲转过身去，偷偷拭去一滴泪。

===Original===
A mother’s tear, that heart’s diamond, more precious than all the pearls of Ophir! I saw it running down her cheek. I ran to the poor woman, and whispered to her, “Mother, if you like, I will stay at home.”

===Chinese===
母亲的一滴泪——那心头落下的钻石，比俄斐（Ophir）所有的珍珠还要珍贵！我看见它顺着她的脸颊淌下。我奔到这可怜的女人身边，低声对她说：「妈妈，要是您愿意，我就留在家里。」

===Original===
“No, no, go, my child,” she said, “Mocquet is right; you must, sooner or later, learn to be a man.”

===Chinese===
「不，不，去吧，我的孩子，」她说，「莫凯说得对；你迟早要学会做一个男子汉。」

===Original===
I gave her another last kiss; then I ran after Mocquet, who had already started.

===Chinese===
我又给了她最后一个吻；随后便追已经先行出发的莫凯去了。

===Original===
After I had gone a few paces, I looked round; my mother had run into the middle of the road, that she might keep me in sight as long as possible; it was my turn now to wipe away a tear.

===Chinese===
走出几步，我回头望去；母亲已跑到路中央，好尽可能久地目送我远去；这一回，轮到我抹去一滴泪了。

===Original===
“How now?” said Mocquet, “you crying too, Monsieur Alexandre!”

===Chinese===
「这是怎么了？」莫凯说，「您也哭啦，亚历山大先生！」

===Original===
“Nonsense, Mocquet! it’s only the cold makes my eyes run.”

===Chinese===
「胡说，莫凯！不过是冷风把眼睛吹得淌水罢了。」

===Original===
But Thou, O God, who gavest me that tear, Thou knowest that it was not because of the cold that I was crying.

===Chinese===
可是，主啊，您既赐下那一滴泪给我，您便知道，我落泪，决不是因为寒冷。

### VIII
===Original===
It was pitch dark when we reached Mocquet’s house. We had a savoury omelette and stewed rabbit for supper, and then Mocquet made my bed ready for me. He kept his word to my mother, for I had a good mattress, two white sheets and two good warm coverlids.

===Chinese===
我们到达莫凯家时，天已经黑得伸手不见五指。我们的晚饭是一份香喷喷的煎蛋卷和一锅炖兔肉，饭后莫凯便为我铺床。他对母亲信守了诺言，因为我的确有一张厚实的床垫、两条雪白的床单和两床暖和的厚被子。

===Original===
“Now,” said Mocquet, “tuck yourself in there, and go to sleep; we may probably have to be off at four o’clock tomorrow morning.”

===Chinese===
「好啦，」莫凯说，「钻进被窝里，睡吧；明天早晨四点钟我们多半就得动身。」

===Original===
“At any hour you like, Mocquet.”

===Chinese===
「随您几时都行，莫凯。」

===Original===
“Yes, I know, you are a capital riser over night, and tomorrow morning I shall have to throw a jug of cold water over you to make you get up.”

===Chinese===
「是啦，我知道，头天夜里您是个顶呱呱的早起人，到了明早，怕是要泼您一壶凉水才叫得起来。」

===Original===
“You are welcome to do that, Mocquet, if you have to call me twice.”

===Chinese===
「您大可那么做，莫凯，只要您得叫上我两回。」

===Original===
“Well, we’ll see about that.”

===Chinese===
「嘿，到时候瞧吧。」

===Original===
“Are you in a hurry to go to sleep, Mocquet?”

===Chinese===
「您急着要睡吗，莫凯？」

===Original===
“Why, whatever do you want me to do at this hour of the night?”

===Chinese===
「怎么？都这大半夜了，您还要我做什么？」

===Original===
“I thought, perhaps, Mocquet, you would tell me one of those stories that I used to find so amusing when I was a child.”

===Chinese===
「我想，莫凯，您兴许肯给我讲一个我小时候最爱听的那种故事呢。」

===Original===
“And who is going to get up for me at two o’clock tomorrow, if I sit telling you tales till midnight? Our good priest, perhaps?”

===Chinese===
「可我要是坐着给您讲故事讲到半夜，明天两点钟谁来叫我起身呢？总不见得是咱们那位好神父吧？」

===Original===
“You are right, Mocquet.”

===Chinese===
「您说得对，莫凯。」

===Original===
“It’s fortunate you think so!”

===Chinese===
「您能这么想，可真是万幸！」

===Original===
So I undressed and went to bed. Five minutes later Mocquet was snoring like a bass viol.

===Chinese===
于是我便脱衣上床。五分钟之后，莫凯已鼾声大作，像一把低音提琴似的轰轰作响。

===Original===
I turned and twisted for a good two hours before I could get to sleep. How many sleepless nights have I not passed on the eve of the first shoot of the season! At last, towards midnight fatigue gained the mastery over me. A sudden sensation of cold awoke me with a start at four o’clock in the morning; I opened my eyes. Mocquet had thrown my bedclothes off over the foot of the bed, and was standing beside me, leaning both hands on his gun, his face beaming out upon me, as, at every fresh puff of his short pipe, the light from it illuminated his features.

===Chinese===
我在床上翻来覆去足有两个钟头才合上眼。开猎前夜，我度过了多少个不眠之夜啊！终于，将近半夜时分，疲乏占了上风。清晨四点，一阵突如其来的寒意把我猛地惊醒；我睁开了眼。莫凯已把我的被褥从床尾掀开，正立在我身旁，双手按在枪上，面容在短烟斗每一次明灭的火光里熠熠地照向我。

===Original===
“Well, how have you got on, Mocquet?”

===Chinese===
「怎么样，事情办得如何了，莫凯？」

===Original===
“He has been tracked to his lair.”

===Chinese===
「它已被追到老巢了。」

===Original===
“The wolf? and who tracked him?”

===Chinese===
「那头狼？谁追到的？」

===Original===
“This foolish old Mocquet.”

===Chinese===
「是这个老糊涂莫凯。」

===Original===
“Bravo!”

===Chinese===
「好极了！」

===Original===
“But guess where he has chosen to take covert, this most accommodating of good wolves!”

===Chinese===
「不过您猜猜，这头最通情达理的好狼，挑了什么地方藏身！」

===Original===
“Where was it then, Mocquet?”

===Chinese===
「那是什么地方，莫凯？」

===Original===
“If I gave you a hundred chances you wouldn’t guess! in the Three Oaks Covert.”

===Chinese===
「给您一百次机会也猜不着！在三橡林（Three Oaks Covert）。」

===Original===
“We’ve got him, then?”

===Chinese===
「那我们可就攥着它了？」

===Original===
“I should rather think so.”

===Chinese===
「那还用说。」

===Original===
The Three Oaks Covert is a patch of trees and undergrowth, about two acres in extent, situated in the middle of the plain of Largny, about five hundred paces from the forest.

===Chinese===
三橡林是一小片树木与丛林，方圆约两英亩，位于拉尔尼（Largny）平原的正中央，离森林约有五百步之遥。

===Original===
“And the keepers?” I went on.

===Chinese===
「那其他看林人呢？」我接着问。

===Original===
“All had notice sent them,” replied Mocquet; “Moynat, Mildet, Vatrin, Lafeuille, all the best shots in short, are waiting in readiness just outside the forest. You and I, with Monsieur Charpentier, from Vallue, Monsieur Hochedez, from Largny, Monsieur Destournelles, from Les Fossés, are to surround the Covert; the dogs will be slipped, the field-keeper will go with them, and we shall have him, that’s certain.”

===Chinese===
「都通知到了，」莫凯答道，「穆瓦纳（Moynat）、米尔德（Mildet）、瓦特兰（Vatrin）、拉弗耶（Lafeuille），一句话，所有那些顶呱呱的神枪手，都已在森林外头待命。您和我，加上瓦吕来的卡尔庞捷先生（Monsieur Charpentier）、拉尔尼来的奥什德先生（Monsieur Hochedez）、莱福塞来的德图尔内勒先生，一同围住这片林子；猎犬一放，护林员跟着进去，这头狼咱们是稳拿了。」

===Original===
“You’ll put me in a good place, Mocquet?”

===Chinese===
「您会把我安排在一个好位置吧，莫凯？」

===Original===
“Haven’t I said that you will be near me; but you must get up first.”

===Chinese===
「我不是说了嘛，您就在我身旁；不过您得先起床。」

===Original===
“That’s true—*Brrou!*”

===Chinese===
「这倒也是——*布噜！*」

===Original===
“And I am going to have pity on your youth and put a bundle of wood in the fireplace.”

===Chinese===
「我可要怜惜您年纪小，往壁炉里添一捆柴了。」

===Original===
“I didn’t dare ask for it; but, on my word of honour, it will be kind of you if you will.”

===Chinese===
「我没敢开口要；不过，凭我的名誉，您要是肯添，那真是再好也没有了。」

===Original===
Mocquet went out and brought in an armful of wood from the timber-yard, and threw it on to the hearth, poking it down with his foot; then he threw a lighted match among the twigs, and in another moment the clear bright flames were dancing and crackling up the chimney. I went and sat on the stool by the fireside, and there dressed myself; you may be sure that I was not long over my toilette; even Mocquet was astonished at my celerity.

===Chinese===
莫凯出去，从柴院里抱了一抱柴进来，扔到炉膛里，用脚把它踏实；然后他往细枝间丢进一根点着的火柴，不消片刻，明亮的火焰便在烟囱里跳跃噼啪作响起来。我走过去，坐在炉边的小凳上，就在那儿穿起了衣裳；诸位可以想见，我这梳洗打扮费不了多少工夫；连莫凯都惊异于我手脚之利索。

===Original===
“Now, then,” he said, “a drop of this, and then off!” And saying this, he filled two small glasses with a yellowish coloured liquor, which did not require any tasting on my part to recognize.

===Chinese===
「那么，」他说，「来一口这个，然后动身！」说着，他斟满两只小杯，杯里是一种微黄的酒液，不劳我尝便认得出来。

===Original===
“You know I never drink brandy, Mocquet.”

===Chinese===
「您知道我从不喝白兰地的，莫凯。」

===Original===
“Ah, you are your father’s son, all over! What will you have, then?”

===Chinese===
「哈，您可真是您父亲的儿子，一个模子刻出来的！那您要喝点什么？」

===Original===
“Nothing, Mocquet, nothing.”

===Chinese===
「什么也不要，莫凯，什么也不要。」

===Original===
“You know the proverb: ‘Leave the house empty; the devil will be there.’ Believe me, you had better put something into your stomach, while I load your gun, for I must keep my promise to that poor mother of yours.”

===Chinese===
「您该知道那句谚语：『屋里空空，魔鬼便来登门。』听我的，您还是往肚里垫点什么的好，正好我替您装枪药，我对我那可怜的令堂许下的诺言可不能不算数。」

===Original===
“Well, then, I will have a crust of bread and a glass of *pignolet*.” *Pignolet* is a light wine made in non-winegrowing districts, generally said to require three men to drink it, one to drink, and two to hold him; I was, however, pretty well accustomed to *pignolet*, and could drink it up without help. So I swallowed my glass of wine while Mocquet loaded my gun.

===Chinese===
「那么好吧，我要一块面包皮和一杯*皮尼奥酒*（*pignolet*）。」皮尼奥酒是一种在不产葡萄的地区酿制的淡酒，据说喝这种酒通常得三个人才对付得了——一个负责喝，另外两个负责按住他；不过我对皮尼奥酒早已相当习惯，独自一人便能把它灌下去。于是我便吞下那杯酒，由莫凯替我装填枪药。

===Original===
“What are you doing, Mocquet?” I asked him.

===Chinese===
「您在做什么，莫凯？」我问他。

===Original===
“Making a cross on your bullet,” he replied. “As you will be near me, we shall probably let fly together, and, although I know you would give me up your share, still, for the glory of it, it will be as well to know which of us killed him, if the wolf falls. So, mind you aim straight.”

===Chinese===
「在您的弹丸上划一个十字，」他答道，「您既在我身边，我们多半会一同开枪；虽说我知道您会让出您那一份功劳，可为了争个名头，要是狼倒下了，到底是谁打死的总该弄个明白。所以，您可要瞄得准。」

===Original===
“I’ll do my best, Mocquet.”

===Chinese===
「我尽力就是，莫凯。」

===Original===
“Here’s your gun, then, loaded for bird-shooting; and now, gun over your shoulder, and off we start.”

===Chinese===
「枪给您，装的是打鸟的霰弹；好了，把枪往肩上一挎，咱们动身。」

### IX
===Original===
The meeting-place was on the road leading to Chavigny. Here we found the keepers and some of the huntsmen, and within another ten minutes those who were missing had also joined us. Before five o’clock struck, our number was complete, and then we held a council of war to decide our further proceedings. It was finally arranged that we should first take up our position round the Three Oaks Covert at some considerable distance from it, and then gradually advance so as to form a cordon round it. Everything was to be done with the utmost silence, it being well known that wolves decamp on hearing the slightest noise. Each of us was ordered to look carefully along the path he followed, to make quite sure that the wolf had not left the covert. Meanwhile the field-keeper was holding Mocquet’s hounds in leash.

===Chinese===
集合的地点在通往沙维尼（Chavigny）的那条路上。我们到了那里，看见看林人和几位猎手已先在；不出十分钟，缺席的人也都到齐了。五点钟敲响之前，人马齐备，于是我们召开了一次「军事会议」，商定下一步如何行事。最终议定：先在距三橡林相当远的地方各就各位，然后逐步推进，把它围成一个圈。一切行动都必须在最寂静中进行，因为众所周知，狼一听见些许动静便会逃遁。每人都奉命仔细察看自己所走的那条小径，确证那头狼不曾离开这片林子。与此同时，护林员正牵着莫凯的猎犬，勒紧了皮带。

===Original===
One by one we took our stand facing the covert, on the spot to which our particular path had conducted us. As it happened, Mocquet and I found ourselves on the north side of the warren, which was parallel with the forest.

===Chinese===
我们一个接一个，各自在所走小径带到的位置上、面向林子站定。巧得很，莫凯和我落在了这片猎苑的北侧，与森林平行的那一边。

===Original===
Mocquet had rightly said that we should be in the best place, for the wolf would in all probability try and make for the forest, and so would break covert on our side of it.

===Chinese===
莫凯方才说我们这一处位置最好，这话一点不错，因为那头狼十有八九会设法扑向森林，于是便要从我们这一侧窜出林子。

===Original===
We took our stand, each in front of an oak tree, fifty paces apart from one another, and then we waited, without moving, and hardly daring to breathe. The dogs on the farther side of the warren were now uncoupled; they gave two short barks, and were then silent. The keeper followed them into the covert, calling halloo as he beat the trees with his stick. But the dogs, their eyes starting out of their heads, their lips drawn back, and their coats bristling, remained as if nailed to the ground. Nothing would induce them to move a step further.

===Chinese===
我们各就各位，每人立在一棵橡树跟前，彼此相隔五十步，随后便一动不动地等候着，连大气也不敢出。猎苑另一头的猎犬此刻被解开了皮带；它们吠了两声短促的叫声，便归于沉寂。看林人随着它们进入林子，一面用棍子敲打着树干，一面高声吆喝。可那群猎犬，眼球凸出、嘴唇翻卷、毛根倒竖，却像被钉在了地上一般。无论怎么哄，它们也不肯再往前迈一步。

===Original===
“Halloa, Mocquet!” cried the keeper, “this wolf of yours must be an extra plucky one, Rocador and Tombelle refuse to tackle him.”

===Chinese===
「喂，莫凯！」看林人喊道，「您这头狼怕是格外凶悍，罗卡多尔（Rocador）和通贝尔（Tombelle）都拒不肯招惹它。」

===Original===
But Mocquet was too wise to make any answer, for the sound of his voice would have warned the wolf that there were enemies in that direction.

===Chinese===
可莫凯深知此理，不肯应一声，因为他一出声便会警觉那头狼：那个方向埋伏着敌人。

===Original===
The keeper went forward, still beating the trees, the two dogs after him cautiously advancing step by step, without a bark, only now and then giving a low growl.

===Chinese===
看林人继续向前，仍旧敲着树干，那两条狗跟在他身后，小心翼翼地一步一步挪动，不吠一声，只是偶尔发出一声低低的咆哮。

===Original===
All of a sudden there was a loud exclamation from the keeper, who called out, “I nearly trod on his tail! the wolf! the wolf! Look out, Mocquet, look out!”

===Chinese===
猛然间，看林人高声惊呼起来，他大叫：「我差点踩到它的尾巴！狼！狼！当心，莫凯，当心！」

===Original===
And at that moment something came rushing towards us, and the animal leapt out of the covert, passing between us like a flash of lightning. It was an enormous wolf, nearly white with age. Mocquet turned and sent two bullets after him; I saw them bound and rebound along the snow.

===Chinese===
就在那一刻，只听得有什么东西朝我们冲来，那头野兽从林中一跃而出，像一道闪电般从我们两人之间掠过。那是一头硕大无朋的狼，因年事已高，毛色几近雪白。莫凯转身朝它连发两弹；我看见弹丸在雪地上左弹右跳。

===Original===
“Shoot, shoot!” he called out to me.

===Chinese===
「开枪，开枪！」他冲我喊。

===Original===
Only then did I bring my gun to the shoulder; I took aim, and fired; the wolf made a movement as if he wanted to bite his shoulder.

===Chinese===
直到那时我才把枪抵上肩头；我瞄准，开火；那头狼做了一个动作，仿佛想回头去咬自己的肩膀。

===Original===
“We have him! we have him!” cried Mocquet, “the lad has hit his mark! Success to the innocent!”

===Chinese===
「中了！中了！」莫凯高呼，「这孩子打中了！愿无辜者得胜！」

===Original===
But the wolf ran on, making straight for Moynat and Mildet, the two best shots in the country round.

===Chinese===
可那头狼依旧往前奔，径直朝穆瓦纳和米尔德——方圆百里最好的两位神枪手——扑去。

===Original===
Both their first shots were fired at him in the open; the second, after he had entered the forest.

===Chinese===
他们两人的头一枪，是在它身处空旷地带时打的；第二枪，是在它窜进森林之后打的。

===Original===
The two first bullets were seen to cross one another, and ran along the ground, sending up spurts of snow; the wolf had escaped them both, but he had no doubt been struck down by the others; that the two keepers who had just fired should miss their aim, was an unheard of thing. I had seen Moynat kill seventeen snipe one after the other; I had seen Mildet cut a squirrel in two as he was jumping from tree to tree.

===Chinese===
只见那两颗先发的弹丸在半空交错，贴着地面飞掠，激起一阵雪雾；那头狼竟从他们两人枪下双双逃脱，但它必定已被旁人打倒了；方才开枪的这两位看林人会失了准头，那是闻所未闻的事。我曾亲眼见穆瓦纳一连打死十七只鹬鸟；我也曾亲眼见米尔德把一只正从一棵树跃向另一棵树的松鼠一枪打成两截。

===Original===
The keepers went into the forest after the wolf; we looked anxiously towards the spot where they had disappeared. We saw them reappear, dejected, and shaking their heads.

===Chinese===
看林人跟着那头狼追进了森林；我们焦急地朝他们消失的那一处张望。只见他们重新现身，神情颓丧，连连摇头。

===Original===
“Well?” cried Mocquet interrogatively.

===Chinese===
「怎么样？」莫凯探询地喊道。

===Original===
“Bah!” answered Mildet, with an impatient movement of his arm, “he’s at Taille-Fontaine by this time.”

===Chinese===
「呸！」米尔德不耐烦地一甩胳膊，答道，「这会儿它早到塔耶-丰丹（Taille-Fontaine）了。」

===Original===
“At Taille-Fontaine!” exclaimed Mocquet, completely taken aback. “What! the fools have gone and missed him, then!”

===Chinese===
「到塔耶-丰丹了！」莫凯惊得目瞪口呆，「怎么！那两个蠢货竟失手了！」

===Original===
“Well, what of that? you missed him yourself, did you not?”

===Chinese===
「那又怎样？您自己不也失手了么？」

===Original===
Mocquet shook his head.

===Chinese===
莫凯摇了摇头。

===Original===
“Well, well, there’s some devilry about this,” he said. “That I should miss him was surprising, but it was perhaps possible; but that Moynat should have shot twice and missed him is not possible, no, I say, no.”

===Chinese===
「嘿，嘿，这里头有鬼，」他说，「我失了手，固然令人诧异，可也并非绝无可能；但穆瓦纳连开两枪竟都失了手，这便绝无可能，不，我说，绝无可能。」

===Original===
“Nevertheless, so it is, my good Mocquet.”

===Chinese===
「可事实偏就是这样，我亲爱的莫凯。」

===Original===
“Besides, you, you hit him,” he said to me.

===Chinese===
「再者，您，您打中了它，」他对我说。

===Original===
“I!… are you sure?”

===Chinese===
「我！……您当真？」

===Original===
“We others may well be ashamed to say it. But as sure as my name is Mocquet, you hit the wolf.”

===Chinese===
「我们这些人，说出来着实羞煞。可我莫凯以性命作保，您打中了那头狼。」

===Original===
“Well, it’s easy to find out if I did hit him, there would be blood on the snow.—Come, Mocquet, let us run and see.” And suiting the action to the word, I set off running.

===Chinese===
「这不难查明我究竟有没有打中它——雪地上该有血迹。——来吧，莫凯，咱们跑去瞧瞧。」我一面说一面已撒开腿跑了出去。

===Original===
“Stop, stop, do not run, whatever you do,” cried Mocquet, clenching his teeth and stamping. “We must go quietly, until we know better what we have to deal with.”

===Chinese===
「站住，站住，千万别跑，」莫凯咬紧牙关、一顿足喊道，「我们必须悄悄地走，直到摸清我们面对的到底是什么为止。」

===Original===
“Well, we will go quietly, then; but at any rate, let us go!”

===Chinese===
「好，那我们就悄悄地走；可无论如何，走吧！」

===Original===
Mocquet then began to follow the wolf’s track, step by step.

===Chinese===
于是莫凯开始一步一步地循着那头狼的踪迹走去。

===Original===
“There’s not much fear of losing it,” I said.

===Chinese===
「这踪迹倒是不愁跟丢。」我说。

===Original===
“It’s plain enough.”

===Chinese===
「够分明的了。」

===Original===
“Yes, but that’s not what I am looking for.”

===Chinese===
「是，可我找的不是这个。」

===Original===
“What are you looking for, then?”

===Chinese===
「那您在找什么？」

===Original===
“You will know in a minute or two.”

===Chinese===
「一会儿您便知道了。」

===Original===
The other huntsmen had now joined us, and as they came along after us, the keeper related to them what had taken place. Meanwhile, Mocquet and I continued to follow the wolf’s footprints, which were deeply indented in the snow. At last we came to the spot where he had received my fire.

===Chinese===
其他的猎手此刻也赶了上来，他们尾随着我们，那看林人一面走一面把方才发生的事对他们述说了一遍。与此同时，莫凯和我继续循着狼的足印走，那些足印在雪地上印得很深。终于，我们来到了它中我那一枪的地方。

===Original===
“There, Mocquet,” I said to him, “you see I did miss him after all!”

===Chinese===
「瞧，莫凯，」我对他说，「您瞧，我到底还是失了手！」

===Original===
“How do you know that you missed him?”

===Chinese===
「您怎么知道失了手？」

===Original===
“Because there are no blood marks.”

===Chinese===
「因为没有血迹。」

===Original===
“Look for the mark of your bullet, then, in the snow.”

===Chinese===
「那您就在雪地上找找您那颗弹丸的痕迹。」

===Original===
I looked to see which way my bullet would have sped if it had not hit the wolf, and then went in that direction; but I tracked for more than a quarter of a mile to no purpose, so I thought I might as well go back to Mocquet. He beckoned to the keepers to approach, and then turning to me, said:—

===Chinese===
我估量着：倘若我那颗弹丸没有击中狼，它会朝哪个方向飞去，便沿着那个方向寻去；可我寻了足有四分之一英里也徒劳无功，于是我想不妨回到莫凯身边去。他示意看林人们走近，然后转向我说：——

===Original===
“Well, and the bullet?”

===Chinese===
「怎么样，那颗弹丸呢？」

===Original===
“I cannot find it.”

===Chinese===
「我找不到。」

===Original===
“I have been luckier than you, then, for I have found it.”

===Chinese===
「那我可比您走运，因为我找到了。」

===Original===
“What, you found it?”

===Chinese===
「怎么，您找到了？」

===Original===
“Right about and come behind me.”

===Chinese===
「转身，跟到我身后去。」

===Original===
I did as I was told, and the huntsmen having come up, Mocquet pointed out a line to them beyond which they were not to pass. The keepers Mildet and Moynat now joined us. “Well?” said Mocquet to them in their turn.

===Chinese===
我依言而行，猎手们也已赶上前来，莫凯给他们指了一道线，叫他们不得越过。看林人米尔德和穆瓦纳这时也来到我们这边。「怎么样？」莫凯轮到问他们。

===Original===
“Missed,” they both answered at once.

===Chinese===
「失手了。」他们俩异口同声地答道。

===Original===
“I saw you had missed him in the open, but when he had reached covert…?”

===Chinese===
「在空旷处失手我看见了，可它窜进了林子之后呢……？」

===Original===
“Missed him there too.”

===Chinese===
「那儿也失手了。」

===Original===
“Are you sure?”

===Chinese===
「你们敢保？」

===Original===
“Both the bullets have been found, each of them in the trunk of a tree.”

===Chinese===
「两颗弹丸都寻到了，各自嵌在一棵树的树干里。」

===Original===
“It is almost past belief,” said Vatrin.

===Chinese===
「这简直难以置信。」瓦特兰说。

===Original===
“Yes,” rejoined Mocquet, “it is almost past belief, but I have something to show you which is even more difficult to believe.”

===Chinese===
「不错，」莫凯应道，「简直难以置信，不过我有一件东西要给你们看，那比这更难以置信。」

===Original===
“Show it us, then.”

===Chinese===
「那就拿出来给我们看看。」

===Original===
“Look there, what do you see on the snow?”

===Chinese===
「瞧那儿，你们在雪地上看见了什么？」

===Original===
“The track of a wolf; what of that?”

===Chinese===
「一头狼的足印；怎么了？」

===Original===
“And close to the mark of the right foot—there—what do you see?”

===Chinese===
「再瞧右脚足印近旁——那儿——你们看见了什么？」

===Original===
“A little hole.”

===Chinese===
「一个小洞。」

===Original===
“Well, do you understand?”

===Chinese===
「那么，你们可明白了？」

===Original===
The keepers looked at each other in astonishment.

===Chinese===
看林人们面面相觑，一脸惊愕。

===Original===
“Do you understand now?” repeated Mocquet.

===Chinese===
「你们现在可明白了？」莫凯又问一遍。

===Original===
“The thing’s impossible!” exclaimed the keepers.

===Chinese===
「这绝不可能！」看林人们叫起来。

===Original===
“Nevertheless it is so, and I will prove it to you.”

===Chinese===
「可事实偏就是如此，我这就证明给你们看。」

===Original===
And so saying, Mocquet plunged his hand into the snow, felt about a moment or two, and then, with a cry of triumph, pulled out a flattened bullet.

===Chinese===
说着，莫凯把手探进雪里，摸索了一两下，便在一声得意的欢呼中，摸出了一颗被拍扁了的弹丸。

===Original===
“Why, that’s my bullet,” I said.

===Chinese===
「咦，这是我那颗弹丸啊。」我说。

===Original===
“You recognise it, then?”

===Chinese===
「您认得它？」

===Original===
“Of course I do, you marked it for me.”

===Chinese===
「那当然，您替我划过记号。」

===Original===
“And what mark did I put on it?”

===Chinese===
「我划的是什么记号？」

===Original===
“A cross.”

===Chinese===
「一个十字。」

===Original===
“You see, sirs,” said Mocquet.

===Chinese===
「诸位瞧见了吧。」莫凯说。

===Original===
“Yes, but explain how this happened.”

===Chinese===
「是瞧见了，可你得解释这是怎么回事。」

===Original===
“This is it; he could turn aside the ordinary bullets, but he had no power over the youngster’s, which was marked with a cross; it hit him in the shoulder, I saw him make a movement as if to try and bite himself.”

===Chinese===
「事情是这样的：他可以拨开寻常的弹丸，可对这个孩子那颗——上面划着十字——他却无能为力；它打中了他的肩膀，我亲眼看见他做了一个仿佛要回头咬自己的动作。」

===Original===
“But,” I broke in, astonished at the silence and amazement which had fallen on the keepers, “if my bullet hit him in the shoulder, why did it not kill him?”

===Chinese===
「可是，」我打断道，对看林人们骤然降临的沉默与惊愕大为不解，「既然我的弹丸打中了他的肩膀，为什么没有把他打死？」

===Original===
“Because it was made neither of gold nor of silver, my dear boy; and because no bullets but those that are made of gold or silver can pierce the skin of the devil, or kill those who have made a compact with him.”

===Chinese===
「因为那颗弹丸既不是金子做的，也不是银子做的，我亲爱的孩子；而能刺穿魔鬼皮肉、能杀死那些与魔鬼订过契约之人的弹丸，唯有金子或银子做成的那一种。」

===Original===
“But, Mocquet,” said the keepers, shuddering, “do you really think…?”

===Chinese===
「可是，莫凯，」看林人们打着寒颤说，「您当真以为……？」

===Original===
“Think? Yes, I do! I could swear that we have had to do this morning with Thibault, the sabot-maker’s wolf.”

===Chinese===
「以为？是的，我当真以为！我可以起誓，今天早晨我们打交道的，正是木屐匠蒂博的那头狼。」

===Original===
The huntsman and keepers looked at one another; two or three of them made the sign of the cross; and they all appeared to share Mocquet’s opinion, and to know quite well what he meant by Thibault’s wolf. I, alone, knew nothing about it, and therefore asked impatiently, “What is this wolf, and who is this Thibault, the sabot-maker?”

===Chinese===
猎手和看林人们彼此对视；其中有两三个人画了个十字；他们似乎都赞同莫凯的看法，也显然完全明白他所说的「蒂博的狼」指的是什么。唯独我对此一无所知，于是不耐烦地问：「这头狼是怎么回事？那个木屐匠蒂博又是何许人也？」

===Original===
Mocquet hesitated before replying, then, “Ah! to be sure!” he exclaimed, “the General told me that I might let you know about it when you were fifteen. You are that age now, are you not?”

===Chinese===
莫凯迟疑了一下才答话，随后，「啊！对了！」他叫起来，「将军曾吩咐过我，等您满了十五岁，便可以把这事告诉您。您如今正好这个年纪，是吧？」

===Original===
“I am sixteen,” I replied with some pride.

===Chinese===
「我十六了。」我带着几分骄傲答道。

===Original===
“Well, then, my dear Monsieur Alexandre, Thibault, the sabot-maker’s wolf, is the devil. You were asking me last night for a tale, were you not?”

===Chinese===
「那么，我亲爱的亚历山大先生，木屐匠蒂博的那头狼，便是魔鬼。您昨儿夜里不是央我讲个故事么？」

===Original===
“Yes.”

===Chinese===
「是的。」

===Original===
“Come back home with me this morning, then, and I will tell you a tale, and a fine one too.”

===Chinese===
「那么，今早随我回家去，我给您讲一个故事，一个顶精彩的故事。」

===Original===
The keepers and huntsmen shook hands with one another in silence and separated, each going his own way; I went back with Mocquet, who then told me the tale which you shall now hear.

===Chinese===
看林人和猎手们默然无言地彼此握手告别，各奔各路；我随莫凯回去，他便在那儿把你们即将听到的这个故事，讲给了我听。

===Original===
Perhaps you will ask me why, having heard it so long ago, I have not told it before. I can only answer you by saying it has remained hidden away in a drawer of my memory, which has remained closed ever since, and which I only opened again three days ago. I would tell you what induced me to do this, but you might, I fear, find the recital somewhat tedious, and as it would take time, I prefer starting at once upon my tale.

===Chinese===
诸位或许要问我，既然这么早就听过了这个故事，为何一直没讲出来。我唯一能回答的是：它一直被收在我记忆中一个抽屉里，那抽屉自此再没打开过，直到三天前我才重新把它拉开。我本可以告诉你们，是什么促使我这么做，可只怕你们会觉得这番叙述颇为乏味，况且也要费些工夫，所以我宁可立刻开始讲我的故事。

===Original===
I say my tale; I ought perhaps to call it Mocquet’s tale—but, upon my word! when you have been sitting on an egg for thirty-eight years, you may be excused for coming to believe at last that you’ve laid it yourself!

===Chinese===
我说「我的故事」；也许我该称它作「莫凯的故事」——可是，凭良心说！当您在一颗蛋上头孵了三十八年，您到头来竟会相信自己把它生下来，这也是情有可原的！

## XII Wolves in the Sheep Fold / XII 羊圈里的狼

===Original===
The forest was not far from the Bailiff’s house, and in two bounds Thibault found himself on the further side of Les Fossés, and in the wooded path leading to the brickyard. He had no sooner entered the forest than his usual escort surrounded him, fawning and blinking with their eyes and wagging their tails to show their pleasure. Thibault, who had been so alarmed the first time he found himself in company with this strange body guard, took no more notice of them now than if they had been a pack of poodles. He gave them a word or two of caress, softly scratched the head of the one that was nearest him, and continued on his way, thinking over his double triumph.

He had beaten his host at the bottle, he had vanquished his adversary at fisticuffs, and in this joyous frame of mind, he walked along, saying aloud to himself:

===Chinese===
执达吏府邸离森林不远，蒂博三跳两跳便到了莱福塞（Les Fossés）那一头，踏上通向砖瓦窑的那条林间小道。他一进林子，那支素日的护卫队便围拢上来，谄媚地眨着眼睛，摇着尾巴，以示欢喜。头一回与这伙古怪的护兵为伍时，蒂博还大惊失色，如今却把它们看得跟一群卷毛狗没什么两样，再不放在心上。他对它们抚慰了一两句，又轻轻搔了搔离他最近那一匹的脑袋，便继续赶路，一面走，一面回味自己那双料凯旋。

他在酒坛子上赢了主人，又在拳脚上打败了对手，他怀着这般得意的心境，边走边高声自语：

===Original===
“You must acknowledge, friend Thibault, that you are a lucky rascal! Madame Suzanne is in every possible respect just what you want! A Bailiff’s wife! my word! that’s a conquest worth making! and if he dies first, what a wife to get! But in either case, when she is walking beside me, and taking my arm, whether as wife or mistress, the devil take it, if I am mistaken for anything but a gentleman! And to think that unless I am fool enough to play my cards badly, all this will be mine! For she did not deceive me by the way she went off: those who have nothing to fear have no need to take flight. She was afraid to show her feelings too plainly at first meeting; but how kind she was after she got home! Well, well, it is all working itself out, as I can see; I have only got to push matters a bit; and some fine morning she will find herself rid of her fat little old man, and then the thing is done. Not that I do, or can, wish for the death of poor Monsieur Magloire. If I take his place after he is no more, well and good; but to kill a man who has given you such good wine to drink! to kill him with his good wine still hot in your mouth! why, even my friend the wolf would blush for me if I were guilty of such a deed.”

===Chinese===
“你得承认，蒂博老伙计，你这混蛋可真有福气！苏珊娜太太（Madame Suzanne）哪一样不是正合你意！一位执达吏太太！好家伙！这可真是值得一搏的彩头！万一他先归了天，娶到手的可就是怎样的媳妇啊！可无论如何，等她傍着我走、挽着我的胳膊的时候，管她是太太还是相好，见鬼，任谁也再不会把我当作什么别的，只当我是个上等人！想想看吧，只要我不是蠢到家、把一手好牌打臭了，这一切可都是我的！她那一走，可没瞒过我：心里没鬼的人用不着溜之大吉。她不过是怕头一回见面就把心思露得太明白；可一回了家，她待我多殷勤啊！好啦，好啦，眼看着这事儿正顺顺当当地水到渠成，我只需再往前推上一把；等哪天清早，她那胖小老头儿一去，这事就成了。倒不是我存心——也不能存心——盼着可怜的马格卢瓦先生归天。等他百年之后我顶了他的缺，那自然好；可要去害死一个请你喝过这般好酒的人！嘴里还留着他那好酒的余温就要去害死他！嗐，我要真干了这种事，连我那位狼朋友都要替我臊得慌。”

===Original===
Then with one of his most roguish smiles, he went on:

“And besides, would it not be as well to have already acquired some rights over Madame Suzanne, by the time Monsieur Magloire passes, in the course of nature, into the other world, which, considering the way in which the old scamp eats and drinks, cannot be a matter of long delay?”

===Chinese===
接着，他脸上浮起那最坏气的一个坏笑，又往下说道：

“再说，等到马格卢瓦先生顺乎天理、寿终正寝地到另一个世界去的时候——瞧这老滑头又吃又喝的那副做派，这天想必也拖不了太久——那时候我岂不是早该先在苏珊娜太太身上挣下几分权利才好？”

===Original===
And then, no doubt because the good qualities of the Bailiff’s wife which had been so highly extolled to him came back to his mind:

“No, no,” he continued, “no illness, no death! but just those ordinary disagreeables which happen to everybody; only, as it is to be to my advantage, I should like rather more than the usual share to fall to him; one cannot at his age set up for a smart young buck; no, everyone according to their dues﻿… and when these things come to pass, I will give you more than a thank you, Cousin Wolf.”

===Chinese===
随后，大约是因为有人在他面前盛赞过的执达吏太太的种种好处又回到了他脑中：

“不，不，”他接着说，“不要病，也不要死！只要那人人难免的寻常灾厄便够了；只不过，既是与我好处攸关，我倒盼着他那一份比寻常人再多上几分；到了他这把年纪，总不能还充什么风流俊后生；不成，人人各按其分……等这些灾厄一桩桩落下来，我自会重重谢你，狼表兄，断不止一句道谢了事。”

===Original===
My readers will doubtless not be of the same way of thinking as Thibault, who saw nothing offensive in this pleasantry of his, but on the contrary, rubbed his hands together smiling at his own thoughts, and indeed so pleased with them that he had reached the town, and found himself at the end of the Rue de Largny before he was aware that he had left the Bailiff’s house more than a few hundred paces behind him.

He now made a sign to his wolves, for it was not quite prudent to traverse the whole town of Villers-Cotterets with a dozen wolves walking alongside as a guard of honour; not only might they meet dogs by the way, but the dogs might wake up the inhabitants.

===Chinese===
笔者相信，读者诸君的想法断不会与蒂博一样。他却把自己这番戏谑看作无伤大雅，非但不以为耻，反而笑吟吟地搓着双手，对自己的盘算颇为得意——得意到这般地步，竟不知不觉已经到了镇上，人已立在拉尔尼街（Rue de Largny）尽头，才发觉自己早把执达吏的宅子甩下了不止几百步。

他朝他的狼群打了个手势，因为要带上一打狼作仪仗，穿行整座维莱-科特雷（Villers-Cotterets）镇子，到底不大稳当；不光一路上可能撞见狗，那些狗还可能把住户吵醒。

===Original===
Six of his wolves, therefore, went off to the right, and six to the left, and although the paths they took were not exactly of the same length, and although some of them went more quickly than the others, the whole dozen nevertheless managed to meet, without one missing, at the end of the Rue de Lormet. As soon as Thibault had reached the door of his hut, they took leave of him and disappeared; but, before they dispersed, Thibault requested them to be at the same spot on the morrow, as soon as night fell.

Although it was two o’clock before Thibault got home, he was up with the dawn; it is true, however, that the day does not rise very early in the month of January.

===Chinese===
于是六匹狼往右、六匹狼往左散去，尽管它们所走的路长短不尽相同，尽管有的比别的走得快些，这一打狼竟还是一匹不缺，齐齐整整地在洛尔梅街（Rue de Lormet）尽头会了齐。蒂博一到自家小屋门口，它们便辞别他散去；不过，散去之前，蒂博嘱咐它们，次日入夜便到原地等候。

蒂博回到家已是两点钟，可天一亮他便起了床；不过也难怪，一月份的天，亮得本就不早。

===Original===
He was hatching a plot. He had not forgotten the promise he had made to the Bailiff to send him some game from his warren; his warren being, in fact, the whole of the forest-land which belonged to his most serene Highness the Duke of Orleans. This was why he had got up in such good time. It had snowed for two hours before daybreak; and he now went and explored the forest in all directions, with the skill and cunning of a bloodhound.

He tracked the deer to its lair, the wild boar to its soil, the hare to its form; and followed their traces to discover where they went at night.

===Chinese===
他正盘算着一桩计议。他没忘记自己对执达吏许下的诺言——要从他的兔苑里送些野味过去；他那兔苑，实则是奥尔良公爵殿下所领的全部森林。正因如此，他才起得这般早。天亮前下了两个时辰的雪；他便如一头寻血猎犬，凭那般本事与狡黠，向四面八方探查整片林子。

他循鹿至其窝，循野猪至其卧处，循野兔至其伏地；又一路追寻它们的足迹，要看清它们夜里都往何处去。

===Original===
And then, when darkness again fell on the Forest, he gave a howl—a regular wolf’s howl, in answer to which came crowding to him the wolves that he had invited the night before, followed by old and young recruits, even to the very cubs of a year old.

Thibault then explained that he expected a more than usually fine night’s hunting from his friends, and as an encouragement to them, announced his intention of going with them himself and giving his help in the chase.

It was in very truth a hunt beyond the power of words to describe. The whole night through did the sombre glades of the forest resound with hideous cries.

===Chinese===
其后，待夜色重又笼罩这片森林，他放声一嗥——一声地道的狼嗥，应声而来的，既有他前夜所邀的那伙狼，又有新老党羽，乃至刚满一岁的狼崽。

蒂博便向它们说明，今夜他指望朋友们帮他猎上一场非比寻常的好猎；为给它们打气，他还宣布自己要亲与其事，从旁助阵。

这一场围猎，当真非笔墨所能形容。整整一夜，森林里那些阴暗的林间空地上，回荡着骇人的嚎叫。

===Original===
Here, a roebuck pursued by a wolf, fell, caught by the throat by another wolf hidden in ambush; there, Thibault, knife in hand like a butcher, was running to the assistance of three or four of his ferocious companions, that had already fastened on a fine young boar of four years old, which he now finished off.

An old she-wolf came along bringing with her half-a-dozen hares which she had surprised in their love frolics, and she had great difficulty in preventing her cubs from swallowing a whole covey of young partridges which the young marauders had come across with their heads under their wings, without first waiting for the wolf-master to levy his dues.

Madame Suzanne Magloire little thought what was taking place at this moment in the forest of Villers-Cotterets, and on her account.

===Chinese===
这一头，一头獐鹿被一匹狼追赶，却被另一匹埋伏的狼一口叼住喉咙而倒下；那一头，蒂博手持尖刀，活像个屠夫，奔去接应三四匹凶残的同伙——它们已然扑倒一头四岁口的壮嫩野猪，他便上前补了最后一刀。

一匹老母狼叼来六只野兔——都是被它撞见于调情嬉闹之中的——它那几只狼崽又撞见一窝小鹧鸪，正把头藏在翅膀底下，险些就要被这帮小毛贼整窝囫囵吞下，老母狼好说歹说才拦住它们，要它们先等狼主爷收足了税再下嘴。

苏珊娜·马格卢瓦太太（Madame Suzanne Magloire）做梦也想不到，此时此刻，在维莱-科特雷的森林里，竟是为着她而闹出这般光景。

===Original===
In a couple of hours’ time the wolves had heaped up a perfect cartload of game in front of Thibault’s hut.

Thibault selected what he wanted for his own purposes, and left over sufficient to provide them a sumptuous repast. Borrowing a mule from a charcoal-burner, on the pretext that he wanted to convey his shoes to town, he loaded it up with the game and started for Villers-Cotterets. There he sold a part of this booty to the gamedealer, reserving the best pieces and those which had been least mutilated by the wolves’ claws, to present to Madame Magloire.

His first intention had been to go in person with his gift to the Bailiff; but Thibault was beginning to have a smattering of the ways of the world, and thought it would, therefore, be more becoming to allow his offering of game to precede him.
